CONTEXT YOU NEED:
- Wayland publishes an MCP server declaration to five CLI agents (WCore, Wayland, Gemini,
  Codex, Claude Code). This takes 20-45 seconds because the agents are serialized.
- Observed bug: on Reconnect of a DISABLED stdio connector, all five agents publish

  agent. The row lands in a state the UI shows as unrecoverable ("publication rollback
  incomplete"), which survives an app restart.
- handleToggleMcpServer PUBLISHES FIRST, THEN commits locally. So during the publication
  window the connector is still enabled:false in durable storage.

# Plan — TVControl full integration in Wayland Desktop (2026-08-04)

**Repo split:** TVControl is DONE (2.2.1 published, tagged `v2.2.1`, verified by clean
install from the public registry: 101 tools over stdio). Every packet below is in
**Wayland Desktop**, worktree `~/dev/wayland-worktrees/packet-attribution`.

**Standing constraints:** no push / merge / tag without Sean. Never commit
`src/process/services/constitution/constitutionFsAuthority.generated.ts`. `AGENTS.md` and
`CLAUDE.md` are dirty from IJFW tooling — not ours, keep them out of every commit.

## W-1 — Publication compare-and-set failure  🔴 BLOCKER

Everything else is cosmetic next to this. It is **not** TVControl-specific: it makes
enabling or reconnecting **any** connector unreliable, and a failure leaves the row in a
state the UI presents as unrecoverable.

### Observed behaviour (reproduced, logs in `/tmp/wl-run4.log`)

Reconnect on a disabled stdio connector:

```
09:49:15.863 [WCoreMcpAgent]   Added MCP server: tvcontrol
09:49:15.912 [WaylandMcpAgent] Installed MCP servers: tvcontrol
09:49:16.852 [GeminiMcpAgent]  Added MCP server: tvcontrol
09:49:34.264 [CodexMcpAgent]   Added MCP server: tvcontrol
09:49:36.311 [ClaudeMcpAgent]  Added MCP server: tvcontrol     <- all five succeed
09:50:22.078 ...               Removed MCP server: tvcontrol   <- rollback, 46s later
```

Publication succeeds everywhere, then the local commit fails and the rollback strips it
back out. Durable result: `status: error`, `lastError: "publication rollback incomplete —
reconnect this connector"`. Survives an app restart.

### ⚠️ Root cause is NOT established — do not skip to a fix

An earlier session attributed this to `refreshServerStatuses` racing the publication.
**That attribution is unproven and probably wrong**: its target filter
(`useMcpConnection.ts:459-466`) requires `s.enabled === true`, and the connector stays
`enabled: false` in durable storage for the whole publication window, because
`handleToggleMcpServer` publishes *before* it commits. It was never a candidate.

Two hypotheses remain. They need different fixes, so identify which one first.

**H1 — row-level guard.** `useMcpServerCRUD.ts:377-385`:

```js
await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
  committed = false;
  return prevServers.map((server) => {
    if (server.id !== serverId || server.updatedAt !== targetServer.updatedAt) return server;
    committed = true;
    return updatedTargetServer;
  });
});
if (!committed) throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while publication was in progress');
```

`targetServer.updatedAt` is read at t0 and compared at t0+45s. Any writer that bumps that
row's `updatedAt` in between makes `committed` false. Full writer list is 16 call sites —
`rg 'saveMcpServers\(' src/renderer`.

**H2 — global revision exhaustion.** `enqueueMcpConfigWrite`
(`useMcpServers.ts:35-61`) CASes on a **whole-config** `revision`, so a write to *any*
server invalidates a write to *every* server. It retries `MAX_MCP_CONFIG_CAS_ATTEMPTS = 16`
then throws `MCP config changed too frequently to commit safely` — which lands in the same
catch and produces the same rollback. Agent-detection sweeps run every few seconds during
publication, so sustained revision churn is plausible.

H1 and H2 are distinguishable by the thrown message alone.

### Phase A — diagnose (do this first, it is cheap)

1. Log every `enqueueMcpConfigWrite` entry: caller tag, attempt number, whether the CAS
   applied, and the target row's `updatedAt` before and after.
2. Reproduce: clean dev profile, add an stdio connector, click Reconnect, capture the log.
   Reproduction recipe is in `.planning/HANDOFF-2026-08-04-tvcontrol.md`.
3. **Deliverable: the named writer and the exact thrown message.** No fix lands before this.

Reproduction is reliable — it fired on every reconnect attempt across three runs, because
five serialized CLI agents take 20-45s to publish.

### Phase B — fix, per finding

- **If H1:** stop comparing a 45-second-old `updatedAt`. Re-read the row inside the updater
  and accept the commit when the declaration is materially unchanged, or move the row guard
  onto an identity that publication owns rather than one every writer bumps.
- **If H2:** the retry budget is not the fix — raise it and it still fails under load.
  Either scope the CAS to the row rather than the whole config, or serialize
  user-initiated publication against background writers.

Whichever it is, a **failed local commit after a fully successful publication must not be
reported as a rollback failure.** Publication succeeded; the correct recovery is to keep
it and re-commit, not to strip five agents and mark the row unrecoverable.

### Acceptance

- Enabling a connector whose publication takes >30s commits and does not roll back.
- Regression test in `tests/unit/useMcpServerCRUD.dom.test.tsx` (71 assertions already
  there): a slow `syncMcpToAgents` with a competing writer underneath it must still commit.
  **The test must fail against current `main`** — prove it before fixing.
- After a genuine publication failure, the row returns to a state the user can retry from.

## W-2 — Land the two Claude Code agent fixes  (already built)

Committed locally as `e3303e5cc`, needs a PR. Independent of W-1, can run in parallel.

Both reproduced against the real CLI, both fixed in
`src/process/services/mcpServices/agents/ClaudeMcpAgent.ts`:

- `claude mcp add-json` is not an upsert — exits 1 with `MCP server X already exists in
  user config`. Only the **stdio** path uses `add-json`; the HTTP/SSE path uses `claude mcp
  add`, which overwrites. So re-publishing any stdio connector to Claude Code always failed
  while the same operation on a remote connector always succeeded. Now removes user-scope
  then re-adds.
- `claude mcp remove` on an absent server exits 1 with `No MCP server named "X" in <scope>
  scope`. The absence check looked for `not found` / `does not exist` **and read
  `error.message`, which `safeExecFile` fixes to `Command failed with exit code 1`** — so no
  wording could ever have matched and rollback always "failed". Now classifies on
  `execErrorDetail`, which carries stderr. `CodexMcpAgent` already did both.

Tests in `tests/unit/claudeMcpAgent.test.ts` assert against the CLI's verbatim stderr for
all three removal scopes, plus a negative control (permission denied, timeout) so neither
predicate can degrade into `() => true`. 7 pass; typecheck clean; 65 MCP unit tests and
71 CRUD DOM tests pass.

Verified live: a connector stuck in the divergent state recovers on reconnect, logging
`[ClaudeMcpAgent] Replaced existing MCP server: tvcontrol`.

---

## W-3 — TVControl as a first-class catalog connector

Do **after** W-1. Installing from the catalog runs the same publication path, so shipping
this first just gives people a broken first-run.

Four files, no new machinery:

- `src/renderer/mcp-catalog/entries/com.ferroxlabs-tvcontrol.json` — copy the shape of
  `entries/ai.perplexity-perplexity-mcp.json`: `registryType: "npm"`,
  `identifier: "@ferroxlabs/tvcontrol"`, `version: "latest"`, `runtimeHint: "npx"`,
  `transport: { type: "stdio" }`, no `environmentVariables` (TVControl needs none).
  `x-wayland`: `license: "MIT"`, `auth.method: "local-credentials"`,
  `platforms: ["macos","windows"]`, categories/tags for trading.
- a row in `src/renderer/mcp-catalog/catalog.json` (107 entries today).
- `src/renderer/mcp-catalog/icons/com.ferroxlabs-tvcontrol.svg`.
- `src/renderer/mcp-catalog/guides/com.ferroxlabs-tvcontrol.md` — **this carries the
  precondition**: TradingView Desktop must be running with a remote debugging port.
  `scripts/launch_tv_debug_mac.sh` / `launch_tv_debug.bat` in the TVControl package do it,
  and the Windows script already handles the MSIX/Store install via `Get-AppxPackage`.

Verified plumbing: `entryToServerData.ts:121` maps that entry to `command: 'npx'`, and
`resolveMcpStdioSpawn` (`mcpStdioSpawn.ts:38-42`) rewrites `npx` to Wayland's bundled Bun —
so it installs and runs on a machine with no system node. The catalog is glob-imported by
`useMcpLibrary.ts:21-33`; no separate build step, `bun run package` picks it up.

**Do NOT bundle it as a builtin** and do not build detection-gating. An earlier
recommendation said detection-gate it; that reasoning was about shipping it as an
always-on builtin, where most users would get a dead tool. A catalog entry is opt-in — the
user clicks Install — so the concern is moot. The catalog schema has no precondition
concept (`x-wayland` supports only `platforms` and `minWaylandVersion`), adding one is a
feature in its own right, and `chrome-devtools`, `playwright` and `apple-mcp` all ship with
host preconditions handled by their setup guide. Be consistent with them.

**Do not flip `TV_MCP_ADVANCED`.** `ui_evaluate` executes arbitrary page JavaScript and is
unregistered by default. The catalog entry must not set it.

## Optional TVControl follow-ups (separate repo, not blocking)

1. **The "offline" suite is not offline.** `tests/state.test.js` connects to a live
   TradingView whenever one is listening on 9222 — it ran for 11 minutes against Sean's
   real chart before being killed. An ordinary `npm test` can mutate a live chart. Same
   shape as the bug just fixed: the tests did not know what they were talking to.
2. **Two moderate advisories remain** in the SDK's HTTP stack (`hono`,
   `@hono/node-server`, `body-parser`). Below the CI threshold and unreachable — TVControl
   only runs stdio. Clearing them means SDK 1.30.0, which deserves its own PR with
   `tests/mcp_stdio.test.js` as the gate, not a drive-by bump. That test is the only thing
   standing between an SDK bump and another silent total outage.

===== SOURCE: useMcpServerCRUD.ts handleToggleMcpServer (the failing CAS) =====
  // Enable/disable MCP server
  const handleToggleMcpServer = useCallback(
    async (serverId: string, enabled: boolean, expectedRevision?: number) => {
      const currentServers = await readMcpServers();
      const targetServer = currentServers.find((server) => server.id === serverId);
      if (!targetServer) return false;
      if (expectedRevision !== undefined && targetServer.updatedAt !== expectedRevision) return false;
      const updatedTargetServer: IMcpServer = {
        ...targetServer,
        enabled,
        updatedAt: nextMcpRevision(targetServer.updatedAt),
        ...(enabled && targetServer.lastError?.includes(MCP_PUBLICATION_DIVERGENCE_MARKER)
          ? { status: 'disconnected', lastError: undefined }
          : {}),
      };
      let externalMutationAttempted = false;

      try {
        if (enabled) {
          // Publish before committing the local enabled state. A failed or
          // partial publication can therefore never leave a false-green row.
          externalMutationAttempted = true;
          await syncMcpToAgents(updatedTargetServer, true);
        } else {
          externalMutationAttempted = true;
          await removeMcpFromAgents(targetServer.name, undefined, targetServer.transport.type);
        }

        let committed = false;
        await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
          committed = false;
          return prevServers.map((server) => {
            if (server.id !== serverId || server.updatedAt !== targetServer.updatedAt) return server;
            committed = true;
            return updatedTargetServer;
          });
        });
        if (!committed) throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while publication was in progress');

        if (enabled) {
          setTimeout(() => void checkSingleServerInstallStatus(targetServer.name), 100);
        } else {
          setAgentInstallStatus((prev) => {
            const updated = { ...prev };
            delete updated[targetServer.name];
            void ConfigStorage.set('mcp.agentInstallStatus', updated).catch(() => {
              // Install-status cache is non-authoritative UI evidence.
            });
            return updated;
          });
        }
        // The exact declaration revision published to the adapters is the
        // reconnect receipt. Callers must probe this object rather than infer a
        // revision from a later storage read that may already be superseded.
        return updatedTargetServer;
      } catch (error) {
        if (externalMutationAttempted) {
          try {
            if (enabled) {
              await removeMcpFromAgents(updatedTargetServer.name, undefined, updatedTargetServer.transport.type);
            } else {
              await syncMcpToAgents(targetServer, true);
            }
          } catch (rollbackError) {
            const rollbackErrors: unknown[] = [rollbackError];
            try {
              // The adapter operation may have survived its failed
              // compensation. Retain a durable, current-row reconciliation
              // handle without overwriting a concurrent declaration edit. If
              // the row was concurrently deleted, recreate only disabled error
              // truth so the unresolved external publication cannot disappear.
              await saveMcpServers((prevServers) =>
                retainMcpPublicationReconciliation(prevServers, serverId, updatedTargetServer)
              );
            } catch (persistenceError) {
              rollbackErrors.push(persistenceError);
            }
            Message.error(enabled ? t('settings.mcpSyncError') : t('settings.mcpRemoveError'));
            const failure = new Error('MCP toggle failed and publication rollback was incomplete', { cause: error });
            Object.assign(failure, { rollbackErrors });
            throw failure;
          }
        }
        Message.error(enabled ? t('settings.mcpSyncError') : t('settings.mcpRemoveError'));
        return false;
      }
    },

===== SOURCE: useMcpServers.ts write queue + global revision CAS =====
// renderer-wide durable mutation queue so those hook instances cannot race and
// overwrite each other's config snapshots.
let mcpConfigWriteQueue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
const mcpConfigListeners = new Set<McpConfigListener>();
const MAX_MCP_CONFIG_CAS_ATTEMPTS = 16;

function publishMcpConfig(servers: IMcpServer[]): void {
  for (const listener of mcpConfigListeners) listener(servers);
}

async function readMcpConfig(): Promise<{ revision: string; servers: IMcpServer[] }> {
  const response = await mcpService.getMcpConfigSnapshot.invoke();
  if (!response.success || !response.data) throw new Error(response.msg || 'Failed to read MCP config');
  return response.data;
}

function enqueueMcpConfigRead(): Promise<IMcpServer[]> {
  const operation = mcpConfigWriteQueue.then(async () => (await readMcpConfig()).servers);
  mcpConfigWriteQueue = operation.then(
    (): void => undefined,
    (): void => undefined
  );
  return operation;
}

function enqueueMcpConfigWrite(serversOrUpdater: IMcpServer[] | ((prev: IMcpServer[]) => IMcpServer[])): Promise<void> {
  const operation = mcpConfigWriteQueue.then(async () => {
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_MCP_CONFIG_CAS_ATTEMPTS; attempt += 1) {
      // oxlint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop -- every retry must observe the previous CAS result
      const persisted = await readMcpConfig();
      const next =
        typeof serversOrUpdater === 'function'
          ? serversOrUpdater(persisted.servers)
          : structuredClone(serversOrUpdater);
      // oxlint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop -- CAS retries are intentionally serialized
      const response = await mcpService.compareAndSetMcpConfig.invoke({
        expectedRevision: persisted.revision,
        nextServers: next,
      });
      if (!response.success || !response.data) throw new Error(response.msg || 'Failed to write MCP config');
      if (!response.data.applied) continue;

      // Publish only the main-process-confirmed snapshot after persistence.
      // A rejected or conflicted write cannot manufacture renderer state.
      publishMcpConfig(response.data.snapshot.servers);
      return;
    }
    throw new Error('MCP config changed too frequently to commit safely');
  });
  mcpConfigWriteQueue = operation.catch((): void => undefined);
  return operation;
}

===== SOURCE: useMcpConnection.ts divergence marker + early return =====
const hasPublicationDivergence = (server: Pick<IMcpServer, 'lastError'>): boolean =>
  typeof server.lastError === 'string' && server.lastError.includes(MCP_PUBLICATION_DIVERGENCE_MARKER);

export function retainMcpPublicationReconciliation(
  servers: IMcpServer[],
  serverId: string,
  fallback: IMcpServer
): IMcpServer[] {
  const exactIndex = servers.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.id === serverId);
  const canonicalIndex = servers.findIndex(
    (candidate) => mcpServerCollisionKey(candidate.name) === mcpServerCollisionKey(fallback.name)
  );
  const reconciliationIndex = exactIndex >= 0 ? exactIndex : canonicalIndex;
  const current = reconciliationIndex >= 0 ? servers[reconciliationIndex] : undefined;
  const reconciliationServer: IMcpServer = {
    ...(current ?? fallback),
    enabled: current?.enabled ?? false,
    status: 'error',
    lastError: MCP_PUBLICATION_DIVERGENCE_ERROR,
    updatedAt: nextMcpRevision((current ?? fallback).updatedAt),
  };

  if (reconciliationIndex < 0) return [...servers, reconciliationServer];
  const next = [...servers];
  next[reconciliationIndex] = reconciliationServer;
  return next;
}

const publicationDivergenceError = (probeError: string): string => {
  const suffix = `; ${MCP_PUBLICATION_DIVERGENCE_MARKER} — reconnect this connector`;
  return `${truncateErrorMessage(probeError, MCP_ERROR_MAX_LENGTH - suffix.length)}${suffix}`;
};

/**
 * MCP standalone-probe management hook.
 * A successful probe proves server reachability and tool discovery only. It
 * never proves that an active chat session received or can invoke those tools.
 */
export const useMcpConnection = (
  mcpServers: IMcpServer[],
  saveMcpServers: (serversOrUpdater: IMcpServer[] | ((prev: IMcpServer[]) => IMcpServer[])) => Promise<void>,
  message: ReturnType<typeof import('@arco-design/web-react').Message.useMessage>[0],
  onAuthRequired?: (server: IMcpServer) => void, // Added: callback fired when authentication is required
  removeMcpFromAgents?: (serverName: string, successMessage?: string, transportType?: string) => Promise<unknown>,
  syncMcpToAgents?: (server: IMcpServer, skipRecheck?: boolean) => Promise<unknown>,
  readMcpServers?: () => Promise<IMcpServer[]>
) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [testingServers, setTestingServers] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  // Connection test function
  const handleTestMcpConnection = useCallback(
    async (server: IMcpServer) => {
      // A probe cannot reconcile a partially-mutated external publication. The
      // explicit reconnect path must first republish the declaration and pass
      // the exact committed revision back here.
      if (hasPublicationDivergence(server)) return;

      setTestingServers((prev) => ({ ...prev, [server.id]: true }));


===== SOURCE: reconnect wiring =====
  // Disable = disable + tear the config out of every agent (no live socket to
  // close; agents reconnect lazily). Reconnect = enable + re-probe. Remove =
  // delete from config + agents.
  const disconnect = useCallback((serverId: string): void => void crud.handleToggleMcpServer(serverId, false), [crud]);
  const reconnect = useCallback(
    async (server: IMcpServer) => {
      // Reconnect is an explicit reconciliation action, even when local truth
      // remained enabled after an incomplete rollback. Republish first, then
      // probe only the exact revision returned by that publication commit.
      const publishedServer = await crud.handleToggleMcpServer(server.id, true);
      if (!publishedServer) return;
      await conn.handleTestMcpConnection(publishedServer);
    },
    [crud, conn]
  );
  const remove = useCallback((serverId: string): void => void crud.handleDeleteMcpServer(serverId), [crud]);

  // On mount + whenever the configured set changes: refresh standalone probe
  // status/tool inventory. This is not active-chat readiness.
  // (non-destructive), refresh per-agent install status, and recompute leftovers.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (mcpServers.length === 0) {
      void computeStale();
      return;
    }
    void conn.refreshServerStatuses(mcpServers);
    void checkAgentInstallStatus(mcpServers);
    void computeStale();
    // refreshServerStatuses/checkAgentInstallStatus are stable callbacks; keyed on
    // the configured set so a newly added/removed server re-resolves.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [mcpServers]);

---
name: ijfw-plan-check
description: "Donahoe Loop audit gate before execution. Trigger: 'audit plan', 'check plan', 'review plan', 'plan audit', 'plan check', 'before we build', 'before execution', 'validate the plan', 'is this plan solid', 'plan review'. Owns pre-execution audit intent -- fires before any foreign plan-checker."
---

Pre-execution audit gate. Runs before EXECUTE. Verdict is decisive.

## Step 1 -- Locate the plan

Check in order: user-specified path, `.ijfw/memory/plan.md`, `.planning/**/PLAN.md`.
If none found: `No plan doc located. Paste the plan or give the path.` Do not proceed.

## Step 2 -- Goal-backward analysis

Read success criteria from `.ijfw/memory/brief.md` if present. For every task: does
it trace to a criterion? Tasks with no traceable criterion are scope drift -- flag them.

## Step 3 -- Scope leak check

Anything in the plan not in the brief is a scope leak. List each with task name + reason.
If no brief exists, flag the absence as a risk.

## Step 4 -- Risk surface

Flag tasks that are under-specified (no verify step, no file path), half-baked
(depends on an undecided decision), or destructive (no rollback note).

## Step 5 -- Dependency ordering

If task B needs task A's output but B is listed before A, flag the inversion with names.

## Step 6 -- Verdict

```
Plan audit: <N> tasks reviewed
Goal alignment:   <N> trace to criteria / <N> need attention
Scope:            clean | <N> leak(s)
Risk surface:     <N> need sharpening
Dependency order: correct | <N> inversion(s)

Verdict: PASS | FLAG | BLOCK

Must-fix before execution (FLAG):
  1. <task> -- <file>:<line> -- <fix>

Rework needed (BLOCK):
  1. <issue> -- <reason>
```

- **PASS**: proceed to EXECUTE.
- **FLAG**: fix numbered items, then proceed.
- **BLOCK**: rework required. Do not execute until re-audited.

Closer: `You have a <PASS|FLAG|BLOCK> -- <next action>.`

## Output contract

Emit a `gate-result` block as the **LAST** content of your output. Nothing
after it. Use `gate="plan-check"`. Statuses: `PASS | CONDITIONAL | WARN | FLAG | FAIL`.

Format:

```gate-result
{
  "schema_version": "1.0",
  "gate": "plan-check",
  "status": "<STATUS>",
  "project_type": "<from project-type-detector>",
  "lenses": [],
  "affected_artifacts": [],
  "accounting": {"duration_ms": 0, "lenses_invoked": 0, "cost_usd": null},
  "remediation": [],
  "receipts_ref": null,
  "supersedes": null,
  "gate_id": "<gate-with-colons-replaced-by-dashes>-<ts>-<rand4>",
  "emitted_at": "<ISO-8601>"
}
```
# RTK - Rust Token Killer (Codex CLI)

**Usage**: Token-optimized CLI proxy for shell commands.

## Rule

Always prefix shell commands with `rtk`.

Examples:

```bash
rtk git status
rtk cargo test
rtk npm run build
rtk pytest -q
```

## Meta Commands

```bash
rtk gain            # Token savings analytics
rtk gain --history  # Recent command savings history
rtk proxy <cmd>     # Run raw command without filtering
```

## Verification

```bash
rtk --version
rtk gain
which rtk
```

     1	import type React from 'react';
     2	import { useCallback } from 'react';
     3	import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
     4	import { Message } from '@arco-design/web-react';
     5	import { ConfigStorage } from '@/common/config/storage';
     6	import type { IMcpServer } from '@/common/config/storage';
     7	import { mcpServerCollisionKey } from '@/common/mcp';
     8	import { acpConversation, mcpService } from '@/common/adapter/ipcBridge';
     9	import { archiveConfiguredMcpServerHttp } from '@/renderer/services/McpConfigService';
    10	import { isElectronDesktop } from '@/renderer/utils/platform';
    11	import { MCP_PUBLICATION_DIVERGENCE_MARKER, retainMcpPublicationReconciliation } from './useMcpConnection';
    12	
    13	function nextMcpRevision(previous?: number): number {
    14	  return Math.max(Date.now(), (previous ?? 0) + 1);
    15	}
    16	
    17	function sameDeclarationRevision(expected: IMcpServer | undefined, actual: IMcpServer | undefined): boolean {
    18	  return expected === undefined
    19	    ? actual === undefined
    20	    : actual !== undefined && actual.id === expected.id && actual.updatedAt === expected.updatedAt;
    21	}
    22	
    23	function newMcpServerId(): string {
    24	  return `mcp_${globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    25	}
    26	
    27	/**
    28	 * MCP server CRUD operations hook.
    29	 * Handles add/edit/delete and enable/disable for MCP servers.
    30	 */
    31	export const useMcpServerCRUD = (
    32	  mcpServers: IMcpServer[],
    33	  saveMcpServers: (serversOrUpdater: IMcpServer[] | ((prev: IMcpServer[]) => IMcpServer[])) => Promise<void>,
    34	  syncMcpToAgents: (server: IMcpServer, skipRecheck?: boolean) => Promise<unknown>,
    35	  removeMcpFromAgents: (serverName: string, successMessage?: string, transportType?: string) => Promise<unknown>,
    36	  checkSingleServerInstallStatus: (serverName: string) => Promise<void>,
    37	  setAgentInstallStatus: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<string, string[]>>>,
    38	  refreshMcpServers: () => Promise<void>,
    39	  readMcpServers: () => Promise<IMcpServer[]> = async () => mcpServers
    40	) => {
    41	  const { t } = useTranslation();
    42	
    43	  // Add MCP server
    44	  const handleAddMcpServer = useCallback(
    45	    async (serverData: Omit<IMcpServer, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>) => {
    46	      const now = Date.now();
    47	      let serverToSync: IMcpServer | null = null;
    48	      const currentServers = await readMcpServers();
    49	      const incomingKey = mcpServerCollisionKey(serverData.name);
    50	      const expectedExisting = currentServers.find((server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey);
    51	      const existingPublished = currentServers.find(
    52	        (server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey && server.enabled
    53	      );
    54	      let revocationAttempted = false;
    55	
    56	      const savedDeclaration: Omit<IMcpServer, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'> = {
    57	        ...serverData,
    58	        enabled: false,
    59	        status: 'disconnected' as const,
    60	        tools: undefined,
    61	        lastConnected: undefined,
    62	        lastError: undefined,
    63	      };
    64	
    65	      // Use functional update to avoid stale-closure issues
    66	      try {
    67	        // Add/import is declaration persistence, never adapter publication. If
    68	        // this replaces an already-enabled definition, revoke that old
    69	        // publication first so storage cannot diverge from an agent config.
    70	        if (existingPublished) {
    71	          revocationAttempted = true;
    72	          await removeMcpFromAgents(existingPublished.name, undefined, existingPublished.transport.type);
    73	        }
    74	        await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
    75	          serverToSync = null;
    76	          const actualExisting = prevServers.find((server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey);
    77	          if (!sameDeclarationRevision(expectedExisting, actualExisting)) {
    78	            throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while add was in progress');
    79	          }
    80	          const existingServerIndex = prevServers.findIndex(
    81	            (server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey
    82	          );
    83	
    84	          if (existingServerIndex !== -1) {
    85	            // If a server with the same name exists, update the existing one
    86	            const updatedServers = [...prevServers];
    87	            updatedServers[existingServerIndex] = {
    88	              ...updatedServers[existingServerIndex],
    89	              ...savedDeclaration,
    90	              updatedAt: nextMcpRevision(updatedServers[existingServerIndex].updatedAt),
    91	            };
    92	            serverToSync = updatedServers[existingServerIndex];
    93	            return updatedServers;
    94	          }
    95	          // If no server with the same name exists, add a new server
    96	          const newServer: IMcpServer = {
    97	            ...savedDeclaration,
    98	            id: newMcpServerId(),
    99	            createdAt: now,
   100	            updatedAt: now,
   101	          };
   102	          serverToSync = newServer;
   103	          return [...prevServers, newServer];
   104	        });
   105	      } catch (error) {
   106	        // A rejected adapter operation may still have mutated a subset. Restore
   107	        // the prior definition to every target whenever revocation was attempted.
   108	        if (revocationAttempted && existingPublished) {
   109	          try {
   110	            await syncMcpToAgents(existingPublished, true);
   111	          } catch (rollbackError) {
   112	            const failure = new Error('Failed to save MCP declaration and restore publication', { cause: error });
   113	            Object.assign(failure, { rollbackErrors: [rollbackError] });
   114	            throw failure;
   115	          }
   116	        }
   117	        throw error;
   118	      }
   119	
   120	      // Check install status
   121	      if (serverToSync) {
   122	        setTimeout(() => void checkSingleServerInstallStatus(serverToSync.name), 100);
   123	      }
   124	
   125	      // Return the newly added/updated server for subsequent connection testing
   126	      return serverToSync;
   127	    },
   128	    [mcpServers, readMcpServers, saveMcpServers, syncMcpToAgents, removeMcpFromAgents, checkSingleServerInstallStatus]
   129	  );
   130	
   131	  // Batch-import MCP servers
   132	  const handleBatchImportMcpServers = useCallback(
   133	    async (serversData: Omit<IMcpServer, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>[]) => {
   134	      const now = Date.now();
   135	      const addedServers: IMcpServer[] = [];
   136	
   137	      const incomingKeys = new Set(serversData.map((server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name)));
   138	      const currentServers = await readMcpServers();
   139	      const expectedByKey = new Map(
   140	        [...incomingKeys].map((key) => [
   141	          key,
   142	          currentServers.find((server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === key),
   143	        ])
   144	      );
   145	      const existingPublished = currentServers.filter(
   146	        (server) => server.enabled && incomingKeys.has(mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name))
   147	      );
   148	      const revocations = await Promise.allSettled(
   149	        existingPublished.map((server) => removeMcpFromAgents(server.name, undefined, server.transport.type))
   150	      );
   151	      const revocationFailures = revocations
   152	        .filter((result): result is PromiseRejectedResult => result.status === 'rejected')
   153	        .map((result) => result.reason);
   154	
   155	      const restoreRevoked = async (): Promise<unknown[]> => {
   156	        // Restore every prior definition, including operations reported as
   157	        // partial failures: rejected wrappers may already have changed agents.
   158	        const restorations = await Promise.allSettled(existingPublished.map((server) => syncMcpToAgents(server, true)));
   159	        return restorations
   160	          .filter((result): result is PromiseRejectedResult => result.status === 'rejected')
   161	          .map((result) => result.reason);
   162	      };
   163	
   164	      if (revocationFailures.length > 0) {
   165	        const restorationFailures = await restoreRevoked();
   166	        throw new AggregateError(
   167	          [...revocationFailures, ...restorationFailures],
   168	          'Failed to revoke existing MCP publications for import'
   169	        );
   170	      }
   171	
   172	      // Use functional update to avoid stale-closure issues
   173	      try {
   174	        await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
   175	          addedServers.length = 0;
   176	          const updatedServers = [...prevServers];
   177	
   178	          for (const incomingKey of incomingKeys) {
   179	            const actual = prevServers.find((server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey);
   180	            if (!sameDeclarationRevision(expectedByKey.get(incomingKey), actual)) {
   181	              throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while import was in progress');
   182	            }
   183	          }
   184	
   185	          serversData.forEach((serverData) => {
   186	            const savedDeclaration: Omit<IMcpServer, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'> = {
   187	              ...serverData,
   188	              enabled: false,
   189	              status: 'disconnected' as const,
   190	              tools: undefined,
   191	              lastConnected: undefined,
   192	              lastError: undefined,
   193	            };
   194	            const incomingKey = mcpServerCollisionKey(serverData.name);
   195	            const existingServerIndex = updatedServers.findIndex(
   196	              (server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey
   197	            );
   198	
   199	            if (existingServerIndex !== -1) {
   200	              // If a server with the same name exists, update the existing one
   201	              updatedServers[existingServerIndex] = {
   202	                ...updatedServers[existingServerIndex],
   203	                ...savedDeclaration,
   204	                updatedAt: nextMcpRevision(updatedServers[existingServerIndex].updatedAt),
   205	              };
   206	            } else {
   207	              // If no server with the same name exists, add a new server
   208	              const newServer: IMcpServer = {
   209	                ...savedDeclaration,
   210	                id: newMcpServerId(),
   211	                createdAt: now,
   212	                updatedAt: now,
   213	              };
   214	              updatedServers.push(newServer);
   215	              addedServers.push(newServer);
   216	            }
   217	          });
   218	
   219	          return updatedServers;
   220	        });
   221	      } catch (error) {
   222	        const restorationFailures = await restoreRevoked();
   223	        if (restorationFailures.length > 0) {
   224	          const failure = new Error('Failed to save MCP import and restore publication', { cause: error });
   225	          Object.assign(failure, { rollbackErrors: restorationFailures });
   226	          throw failure;
   227	        }
   228	        throw error;
   229	      }
   230	
   231	      // Check install status
   232	      setTimeout(() => {
   233	        serversData.forEach((serverData) => {
   234	          void checkSingleServerInstallStatus(serverData.name);
   235	        });
   236	      }, 100);
   237	
   238	      // Return list of newly added servers for subsequent connection testing
   239	      return addedServers;
   240	    },
   241	    [mcpServers, readMcpServers, saveMcpServers, syncMcpToAgents, removeMcpFromAgents, checkSingleServerInstallStatus]
   242	  );
   243	
   244	  // Edit MCP server
   245	  const handleEditMcpServer = useCallback(
   246	    async (
   247	      editingMcpServer: IMcpServer | undefined,
   248	      serverData: Omit<IMcpServer, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>
   249	    ): Promise<IMcpServer | undefined> => {
   250	      if (!editingMcpServer) return undefined;
   251	
   252	      const updatedServer: IMcpServer = {
   253	        ...editingMcpServer,
   254	        ...serverData,
   255	        updatedAt: nextMcpRevision(editingMcpServer.updatedAt),
   256	      };
   257	
   258	      // Treat an edit as an agent-publication transaction. Revoke the old
   259	      // definition first so renames and transport changes cannot strand an
   260	      // orphan config, then publish the replacement. If either step fails,
   261	      // best-effort restore the old enabled definition and keep local storage
   262	      // unchanged so the user has an honest retry surface.
   263	      let oldRevocationAttempted = false;
   264	      let replacementPublicationAttempted = false;
   265	      try {
   266	        if (editingMcpServer.enabled) {
   267	          oldRevocationAttempted = true;
   268	          await removeMcpFromAgents(editingMcpServer.name, undefined, editingMcpServer.transport.type);
   269	        }
   270	        if (updatedServer.enabled) {
   271	          replacementPublicationAttempted = true;
   272	          await syncMcpToAgents(updatedServer, true);
   273	        }
   274	        let committed = false;
   275	        await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
   276	          committed = false;
   277	          return prevServers.map((server) => {
   278	            if (server.id !== editingMcpServer.id || server.updatedAt !== editingMcpServer.updatedAt) return server;
   279	            committed = true;
   280	            return updatedServer;
   281	          });
   282	        });
   283	        if (!committed) throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while edit publication was in progress');
   284	      } catch (error) {
   285	        const rollbackErrors: unknown[] = [];
   286	        if (replacementPublicationAttempted) {
   287	          try {
   288	            await removeMcpFromAgents(updatedServer.name, undefined, updatedServer.transport.type);
   289	          } catch (rollbackError) {
   290	            rollbackErrors.push(rollbackError);
   291	          }
   292	        }
   293	        if (oldRevocationAttempted) {
   294	          try {
   295	            await syncMcpToAgents(editingMcpServer, true);
   296	          } catch (rollbackError) {
   297	            rollbackErrors.push(rollbackError);
   298	          }
   299	        }
   300	        Message.error(t('settings.mcpSyncError'));
   301	        if (rollbackErrors.length > 0) {
   302	          const failure = new Error('MCP edit failed and publication rollback was incomplete', { cause: error });
   303	          Object.assign(failure, { rollbackErrors });
   304	          throw failure;
   305	        }
   306	        throw error;
   307	      }
   308	
   309	      Message.success(t('settings.mcpImportSuccess'));
   310	      // Immediately re-check install status for this server after editing (install status only)
   311	      setTimeout(() => void checkSingleServerInstallStatus(serverData.name), 100);
   312	
   313	      // Return the updated server object for subsequent connection testing
   314	      return updatedServer;
   315	    },
   316	    [saveMcpServers, syncMcpToAgents, removeMcpFromAgents, t, checkSingleServerInstallStatus]
   317	  );
   318	
   319	  // Delete MCP server
   320	  const handleDeleteMcpServer = useCallback(
   321	    async (serverId: string) => {
   322	      const targetServer = mcpServers.find((server) => server.id === serverId);
   323	      if (!targetServer) return;
   324	
   325	      try {
   326	        const agents = await acpConversation.getAvailableAgents.invoke();
   327	        if (!agents.success || !agents.data) throw new Error(agents.msg || t('settings.mcpSyncFailedNoAgents'));
   328	        const archived = isElectronDesktop()
   329	          ? await mcpService.archiveConfiguredServer.invoke({ serverId, agents: agents.data })
   330	          : await archiveConfiguredMcpServerHttp(serverId);
   331	        if (!archived.success) throw new Error(archived.msg || t('settings.mcpDeleteError'));
   332	        await refreshMcpServers();
   333	        Message.success(t('settings.mcpArchivedWithCleanup', 'Connector archived and removed from your agents.'));
   334	
   335	        setAgentInstallStatus((prev) => {
   336	          const updated = { ...prev };
   337	          delete updated[targetServer.name];
   338	          void ConfigStorage.set('mcp.agentInstallStatus', updated).catch(() => {});
   339	          return updated;
   340	        });
   341	      } catch {
   342	        Message.error(t('settings.mcpDeleteError'));
   343	      }
   344	    },
   345	    [mcpServers, setAgentInstallStatus, refreshMcpServers, t]
   346	  );
   347	
   348	  // Enable/disable MCP server
   349	  const handleToggleMcpServer = useCallback(
   350	    async (serverId: string, enabled: boolean, expectedRevision?: number) => {
   351	      const currentServers = await readMcpServers();
   352	      const targetServer = currentServers.find((server) => server.id === serverId);
   353	      if (!targetServer) return false;
   354	      if (expectedRevision !== undefined && targetServer.updatedAt !== expectedRevision) return false;
   355	      const updatedTargetServer: IMcpServer = {
   356	        ...targetServer,
   357	        enabled,
   358	        updatedAt: nextMcpRevision(targetServer.updatedAt),
   359	        ...(enabled && targetServer.lastError?.includes(MCP_PUBLICATION_DIVERGENCE_MARKER)
   360	          ? { status: 'disconnected', lastError: undefined }
   361	          : {}),
   362	      };
   363	      let externalMutationAttempted = false;
   364	
   365	      try {
   366	        if (enabled) {
   367	          // Publish before committing the local enabled state. A failed or
   368	          // partial publication can therefore never leave a false-green row.
   369	          externalMutationAttempted = true;
   370	          await syncMcpToAgents(updatedTargetServer, true);
   371	        } else {
   372	          externalMutationAttempted = true;
   373	          await removeMcpFromAgents(targetServer.name, undefined, targetServer.transport.type);
   374	        }
   375	
   376	        let committed = false;
   377	        await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
   378	          committed = false;
   379	          return prevServers.map((server) => {
   380	            if (server.id !== serverId || server.updatedAt !== targetServer.updatedAt) return server;
   381	            committed = true;
   382	            return updatedTargetServer;
   383	          });
   384	        });
   385	        if (!committed) throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while publication was in progress');
   386	
   387	        if (enabled) {
   388	          setTimeout(() => void checkSingleServerInstallStatus(targetServer.name), 100);
   389	        } else {
   390	          setAgentInstallStatus((prev) => {
   391	            const updated = { ...prev };
   392	            delete updated[targetServer.name];
   393	            void ConfigStorage.set('mcp.agentInstallStatus', updated).catch(() => {
   394	              // Install-status cache is non-authoritative UI evidence.
   395	            });
   396	            return updated;
   397	          });
   398	        }
   399	        // The exact declaration revision published to the adapters is the
   400	        // reconnect receipt. Callers must probe this object rather than infer a
   401	        // revision from a later storage read that may already be superseded.
   402	        return updatedTargetServer;
   403	      } catch (error) {
   404	        if (externalMutationAttempted) {
   405	          try {
   406	            if (enabled) {
   407	              await removeMcpFromAgents(updatedTargetServer.name, undefined, updatedTargetServer.transport.type);
   408	            } else {
   409	              await syncMcpToAgents(targetServer, true);
   410	            }
   411	          } catch (rollbackError) {
   412	            const rollbackErrors: unknown[] = [rollbackError];
   413	            try {
   414	              // The adapter operation may have survived its failed
   415	              // compensation. Retain a durable, current-row reconciliation
   416	              // handle without overwriting a concurrent declaration edit. If
   417	              // the row was concurrently deleted, recreate only disabled error
   418	              // truth so the unresolved external publication cannot disappear.
   419	              await saveMcpServers((prevServers) =>
   420	                retainMcpPublicationReconciliation(prevServers, serverId, updatedTargetServer)
   421	              );
   422	            } catch (persistenceError) {
   423	              rollbackErrors.push(persistenceError);
   424	            }
   425	            Message.error(enabled ? t('settings.mcpSyncError') : t('settings.mcpRemoveError'));
   426	            const failure = new Error('MCP toggle failed and publication rollback was incomplete', { cause: error });
   427	            Object.assign(failure, { rollbackErrors });
   428	            throw failure;
   429	          }
   430	        }
   431	        Message.error(enabled ? t('settings.mcpSyncError') : t('settings.mcpRemoveError'));
   432	        return false;
   433	      }
   434	    },
   435	    [
   436	      mcpServers,
   437	      readMcpServers,
   438	      saveMcpServers,
   439	      syncMcpToAgents,
   440	      removeMcpFromAgents,
   441	      checkSingleServerInstallStatus,
   442	      setAgentInstallStatus,
   443	      t,
   444	    ]
   445	  );
   446	
   447	  return {
   448	    handleAddMcpServer,
   449	    handleBatchImportMcpServers,
   450	    handleEditMcpServer,
   451	    handleDeleteMcpServer,
   452	    handleToggleMcpServer,
   453	  };
   454	};
     1	import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
     2	import type { IMcpServer } from '@/common/config/storage';
     3	import { ipcBridge } from '@/common';
     4	import { mcpService } from '@/common/adapter/ipcBridge';
     5	import { migrateExistingServers } from './migrateExistingServers';
     6	
     7	type McpConfigListener = (servers: IMcpServer[]) => void;
     8	
     9	// MCP settings are rendered from several independent screens. Keep one
    10	// renderer-wide durable mutation queue so those hook instances cannot race and
    11	// overwrite each other's config snapshots.
    12	let mcpConfigWriteQueue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
    13	const mcpConfigListeners = new Set<McpConfigListener>();
    14	const MAX_MCP_CONFIG_CAS_ATTEMPTS = 16;
    15	
    16	function publishMcpConfig(servers: IMcpServer[]): void {
    17	  for (const listener of mcpConfigListeners) listener(servers);
    18	}
    19	
    20	async function readMcpConfig(): Promise<{ revision: string; servers: IMcpServer[] }> {
    21	  const response = await mcpService.getMcpConfigSnapshot.invoke();
    22	  if (!response.success || !response.data) throw new Error(response.msg || 'Failed to read MCP config');
    23	  return response.data;
    24	}
    25	
    26	function enqueueMcpConfigRead(): Promise<IMcpServer[]> {
    27	  const operation = mcpConfigWriteQueue.then(async () => (await readMcpConfig()).servers);
    28	  mcpConfigWriteQueue = operation.then(
    29	    (): void => undefined,
    30	    (): void => undefined
    31	  );
    32	  return operation;
    33	}
    34	
    35	function enqueueMcpConfigWrite(serversOrUpdater: IMcpServer[] | ((prev: IMcpServer[]) => IMcpServer[])): Promise<void> {
    36	  const operation = mcpConfigWriteQueue.then(async () => {
    37	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_MCP_CONFIG_CAS_ATTEMPTS; attempt += 1) {
    38	      // oxlint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop -- every retry must observe the previous CAS result
    39	      const persisted = await readMcpConfig();
    40	      const next =
    41	        typeof serversOrUpdater === 'function'
    42	          ? serversOrUpdater(persisted.servers)
    43	          : structuredClone(serversOrUpdater);
    44	      // oxlint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop -- CAS retries are intentionally serialized
    45	      const response = await mcpService.compareAndSetMcpConfig.invoke({
    46	        expectedRevision: persisted.revision,
    47	        nextServers: next,
    48	      });
    49	      if (!response.success || !response.data) throw new Error(response.msg || 'Failed to write MCP config');
    50	      if (!response.data.applied) continue;
    51	
    52	      // Publish only the main-process-confirmed snapshot after persistence.
    53	      // A rejected or conflicted write cannot manufacture renderer state.
    54	      publishMcpConfig(response.data.snapshot.servers);
    55	      return;
    56	    }
    57	    throw new Error('MCP config changed too frequently to commit safely');
    58	  });
    59	  mcpConfigWriteQueue = operation.catch((): void => undefined);
    60	  return operation;
    61	}
    62	
    63	/**
    64	 * MCP server state management hook.
    65	 * Manages loading, saving, and updating the MCP server list.
    66	 * Includes both user-configured MCP servers and extension-contributed MCP servers.
    67	 */
    68	export const useMcpServers = () => {
    69	  const [mcpServers, setMcpServers] = useState<IMcpServer[]>([]);
    70	  /** Extension-contributed MCP servers (read-only, from extensions) */
    71	  const [extensionMcpServers, setExtensionMcpServers] = useState<IMcpServer[]>([]);
    72	
    73	  useEffect(() => {
    74	    const listener: McpConfigListener = (servers) => setMcpServers(servers);
    75	    mcpConfigListeners.add(listener);
    76	    return () => {
    77	      mcpConfigListeners.delete(listener);
    78	    };
    79	  }, []);
    80	
    81	  const refreshMcpServers = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {
    82	    const data = await enqueueMcpConfigRead();
    83	    // One-time, idempotent migration: tag any server without an explicit
    84	    // `source` as `source: 'custom'` so the new MCP Library Installed page
    85	    // groups pre-library installs under "Custom".
    86	    const migrated = migrateExistingServers(data);
    87	    const changed = migrated.some((server, idx) => server !== data[idx]);
    88	    if (changed) {
    89	      await enqueueMcpConfigWrite((persisted) => migrateExistingServers(persisted));
    90	    } else {
    91	      setMcpServers(migrated);
    92	    }
    93	  }, []);
    94	
    95	  const readMcpServers = useCallback(async (): Promise<IMcpServer[]> => enqueueMcpConfigRead(), []);
    96	
    97	  // Load MCP server configuration
    98	  useEffect(() => {
    99	    void refreshMcpServers().catch((error) => {
   100	      console.error('[useMcpServers] Failed to load MCP config:', error);
   101	    });
   102	
   103	    // Load extension-contributed MCP servers
   104	    void ipcBridge.extensions.getMcpServers
   105	      .invoke()
   106	      .then((extServers) => {
   107	        if (extServers && extServers.length > 0) {
   108	          const converted: IMcpServer[] = extServers.map((s) => ({
   109	            id: String(s.id || ''),
   110	            name: String(s.name || ''),
   111	            description: s.description as string | undefined,
   112	            enabled: s.enabled !== false,
   113	            transport: s.transport as IMcpServer['transport'],
   114	            // No status: an extension manifest declares a connector; it does
   115	            // not prove that this server registered tools in the current chat.
   116	            createdAt: (s.createdAt as number) || Date.now(),
   117	            updatedAt: (s.updatedAt as number) || Date.now(),
   118	            originalJson: String(s.originalJson || '{}'),
   119	            _source: 'extension' as const,
   120	            _extensionName: s._extensionName as string | undefined,
   121	          })) as IMcpServer[];
   122	          setExtensionMcpServers(converted);
   123	        }
   124	      })
   125	      .catch((error) => {
   126	        console.error('[useMcpServers] Failed to load extension MCP servers:', error);
   127	      });
   128	  }, [refreshMcpServers]);
   129	
   130	  // Save MCP server configuration (user-configured only; extension servers are not persisted)
   131	  const saveMcpServers = useCallback(
   132	    (serversOrUpdater: IMcpServer[] | ((prev: IMcpServer[]) => IMcpServer[])) =>
   133	      enqueueMcpConfigWrite(serversOrUpdater),
   134	    []
   135	  );
   136	
   137	  // Combined complete list (user-configured + extension-contributed)
   138	  const allMcpServers = [...mcpServers, ...extensionMcpServers];
   139	
   140	  return {
   141	    mcpServers,
   142	    allMcpServers,
   143	    extensionMcpServers,
   144	    setMcpServers,
   145	    saveMcpServers,
   146	    readMcpServers,
   147	    refreshMcpServers,
   148	  };
   149	};

   130	 * MCP standalone-probe management hook.
   131	 * A successful probe proves server reachability and tool discovery only. It
   132	 * never proves that an active chat session received or can invoke those tools.
   133	 */
   134	export const useMcpConnection = (
   135	  mcpServers: IMcpServer[],
   136	  saveMcpServers: (serversOrUpdater: IMcpServer[] | ((prev: IMcpServer[]) => IMcpServer[])) => Promise<void>,
   137	  message: ReturnType<typeof import('@arco-design/web-react').Message.useMessage>[0],
   138	  onAuthRequired?: (server: IMcpServer) => void, // Added: callback fired when authentication is required
   139	  removeMcpFromAgents?: (serverName: string, successMessage?: string, transportType?: string) => Promise<unknown>,
   140	  syncMcpToAgents?: (server: IMcpServer, skipRecheck?: boolean) => Promise<unknown>,
   141	  readMcpServers?: () => Promise<IMcpServer[]>
   142	) => {
   143	  const { t } = useTranslation();
   144	  const [testingServers, setTestingServers] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
   145	
   146	  // Connection test function
   147	  const handleTestMcpConnection = useCallback(
   148	    async (server: IMcpServer) => {
   149	      // A probe cannot reconcile a partially-mutated external publication. The
   150	      // explicit reconnect path must first republish the declaration and pass
   151	      // the exact committed revision back here.
   152	      if (hasPublicationDivergence(server)) return;
   153	
   154	      setTestingServers((prev) => ({ ...prev, [server.id]: true }));
   155	
   156	      // Update server status - use the unified save function to avoid race conditions
   157	      const updateServerStatus = async (
   158	        status: IMcpServer['status'],
   159	        additionalData?: Partial<IMcpServer>,
   160	        preserveRevision = false
   161	      ): Promise<{ outcome: 'applied' | 'conflict' | 'error'; winner?: IMcpServer }> => {
   162	        let applied = false;
   163	        let winner: IMcpServer | undefined;
   164	        try {
   165	          await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
   166	            applied = false;
   167	            winner =
   168	              prevServers.find((candidate) => candidate.id === server.id) ??
   169	              prevServers.find(
   170	                (candidate) => mcpServerCollisionKey(candidate.name) === mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name)
   171	              );
   172	            return prevServers.map((s) => {
   173	              if (s.id !== server.id || s.updatedAt !== server.updatedAt) return s;
   174	              applied = true;
   175	              return {
   176	                ...s,
   177	                status,
   178	                updatedAt: preserveRevision ? s.updatedAt : nextMcpRevision(s.updatedAt),
   179	                ...additionalData,
   180	              };
   181	            });
   182	          });
   183	        } catch (error) {
   184	          console.error('Failed to update server status:', error);
   185	          return { outcome: 'error' };
   186	        }
   187	        return { outcome: applied ? 'applied' : 'conflict', winner };
   188	      };
   189	
   190	      const reconcileLostProbeFailureCas = async (
   191	        winner: IMcpServer | undefined,
   192	        adapterState: 'revoked' | 'published-original' | 'unknown'
   193	      ): Promise<void> => {
   194	        if (!removeMcpFromAgents || !syncMcpToAgents) return;
   195	
   196	        const findDurableWinner = (servers: IMcpServer[]): IMcpServer | undefined =>
   197	          servers.find((candidate) => candidate.id === server.id) ??
   198	          servers.find((candidate) => mcpServerCollisionKey(candidate.name) === mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name));
   199	        const readDurableWinner = async (): Promise<IMcpServer | undefined> => {
   200	          if (readMcpServers) return findDurableWinner(await readMcpServers());
   201	
   202	          // Tests and legacy callers may not provide a persistence reader. A
   203	          // no-op update still observes the storage layer's current CAS input;
   204	          // the render-time mcpServers array is not authoritative after awaits.
   205	          let durableWinner: IMcpServer | undefined;
   206	          await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
   207	            durableWinner = findDurableWinner(prevServers);
   208	            return prevServers;
   209	          });
   210	          return durableWinner;
   211	        };
   212	        const sameDurableRevision = (left: IMcpServer | undefined, right: IMcpServer | undefined): boolean =>
   213	          left?.id === right?.id &&
   214	          left?.updatedAt === right?.updatedAt &&
   215	          left?.enabled === right?.enabled &&
   216	          (left === undefined ||
   217	            right === undefined ||
   218	            mcpServerCollisionKey(left.name) === mcpServerCollisionKey(right.name));
   219	
   220	        let desired = winner;
   221	        let lastPublished: IMcpServer | undefined;
   222	        try {
   223	          let originalKeyRemoved = adapterState === 'revoked';
   224	          let renameCleanupDone = false;
   225	
   226	          // Every adapter mutation is followed by a fresh durable read. Four
   227	          // attempts bound an actively changing declaration; exhaustion is
   228	          // persisted as divergence instead of publishing an arbitrary loser.
   229	          const reconcileAttempt = async (attempt: number): Promise<boolean> => {
   230	            if (attempt >= 4) return false;
   231	
   232	            const beforeMutation = await readDurableWinner();
   233	            if (!sameDurableRevision(beforeMutation, desired)) desired = beforeMutation;
   234	
   235	            if (lastPublished && !sameDurableRevision(lastPublished, desired)) {
   236	              await removeMcpFromAgents(lastPublished.name, undefined, lastPublished.transport.type);
   237	              lastPublished = undefined;
   238	              const afterStaleCleanup = await readDurableWinner();
   239	              if (!sameDurableRevision(afterStaleCleanup, desired)) {
   240	                desired = afterStaleCleanup;
   241	                return reconcileAttempt(attempt + 1);
   242	              }
   243	            }
   244	
   245	            if (desired?.enabled) {
   246	              // A rename can strand the original config under a different key.
   247	              // Remove it before publishing the exact durable winner.
   248	              if (!renameCleanupDone && desired.name !== server.name) {
   249	                await removeMcpFromAgents(server.name, undefined, server.transport.type);
   250	                originalKeyRemoved = true;
   251	                renameCleanupDone = true;
   252	                const afterOriginalCleanup = await readDurableWinner();
   253	                if (!sameDurableRevision(afterOriginalCleanup, desired)) {
   254	                  desired = afterOriginalCleanup;
   255	                  return reconcileAttempt(attempt + 1);
   256	                }
   257	              }
   258	
   259	              // Multi-adapter publication is not atomic. Record the exact
   260	              // candidate before dispatch so a partial mutation followed by a
   261	              // rejected promise is still revoked by the catch path.
   262	              lastPublished = desired;
   263	              await syncMcpToAgents(desired, true);
   264	            } else if (!originalKeyRemoved) {
   265	              // Disabled/deleted durable truth requires confirmed absence. A
   266	              // previous rejected revocation or restoration may have left any
   267	              // subset of adapters carrying the original definition.
   268	              await removeMcpFromAgents(server.name, undefined, server.transport.type);
   269	              originalKeyRemoved = true;
   270	            }
   271	
   272	            const afterMutation = await readDurableWinner();
   273	            if (sameDurableRevision(afterMutation, desired)) return true;
   274	            desired = afterMutation;
   275	            return reconcileAttempt(attempt + 1);
   276	          };
   277	
   278	          if (await reconcileAttempt(0)) return;
   279	
   280	          // Exhaustion must not leave the last known loser callable. Cleanup
   281	          // is best-effort: a rejection still flows through the catch below,
   282	          // which records durable divergence against current truth.
   283	          if (lastPublished) {
   284	            await removeMcpFromAgents(lastPublished.name, undefined, lastPublished.transport.type);
   285	            lastPublished = undefined;
   286	          }
   287	          await saveMcpServers((prevServers) =>
   288	            retainMcpPublicationReconciliation(prevServers, server.id, desired ?? winner ?? server)
   289	          );
   290	        } catch (reconciliationError) {
   291	          const rollbackErrors: unknown[] = [reconciliationError];
   292	          if (lastPublished) {
   293	            try {
   294	              await removeMcpFromAgents(lastPublished.name, undefined, lastPublished.transport.type);
   295	              lastPublished = undefined;
   296	            } catch (cleanupError) {
   297	              rollbackErrors.push(cleanupError);
   298	            }
   299	          }
   300	
   301	          const fallback = desired ?? winner ?? server;
   302	          try {
   303	            await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => retainMcpPublicationReconciliation(prevServers, server.id, fallback));
   304	          } catch (persistenceError) {
   305	            rollbackErrors.push(persistenceError);
   306	            const failure = new Error('MCP probe reconciliation could not persist publication divergence', {
   307	              cause: reconciliationError,
   308	            });
   309	            Object.assign(failure, { rollbackErrors });
   310	            throw failure;
   311	          }
   312	          console.error('MCP publication reconciliation retained fail-closed divergence:', {
   313	            errorCount: rollbackErrors.length,
   314	            errorTypes: rollbackErrors.map((error) => (error instanceof Error ? 'Error' : typeof error)),
   315	          });
   316	        }
   317	      };
   318	
   319	      const recordProbeFailure = async (errorMsg: string): Promise<void> => {
   320	        let publicationRevoked = !server.enabled;
   321	        let adapterState: 'revoked' | 'published-original' | 'unknown' = server.enabled ? 'unknown' : 'revoked';
   322	        let surfacedError = errorMsg;
   323	        if (server.enabled && removeMcpFromAgents && syncMcpToAgents) {
   324	          try {
   325	            await removeMcpFromAgents(server.name, undefined, server.transport.type);
   326	            publicationRevoked = true;
   327	            adapterState = 'revoked';
   328	          } catch (revocationError) {
   329	            // A rejected removal may have changed a subset of adapters. Restore
   330	            // the previous enabled definition everywhere and keep local enabled
   360	        }
   361	        await globalMessageQueue.add(() => {
   362	          message.error({ content: `${server.name}: ${surfacedError}`, duration: 5000 });
   363	        });
   364	      };
   365	
   366	      try {
   367	        // Keep updatedAt as the declaration revision while the probe runs. Every
   368	        // terminal write below is compare-and-set against that revision, so an
   369	        // edit/re-import racing the probe wins and stale evidence is discarded.
   370	        // This belongs inside the try/finally so even a rejected initial write
   371	        // clears the renderer's in-flight indicator.
   372	        if ((await updateServerStatus('testing', undefined, true)).outcome !== 'applied') return;
   373	
   374	        const response = await mcpService.testMcpConnection.invoke(server);
   375	
   376	        if (response.success && response.data) {
   377	          const result = response.data;
   378	          const truth = readCorrelatedMcpPrepublicationTruth(server, result);
   379	
   380	          // Check whether authentication is required
   381	          if (truth.state === 'authentication-required') {
   382	            // Needing auth is not a connection error - clear any stale lastError
   383	            // so a previous transport failure can't resurface as the reason.
   384	            if ((await updateServerStatus('disconnected', { lastError: undefined })).outcome !== 'applied') return;
   385	            await globalMessageQueue.add(() => {
   386	              message.warning(`${server.name}: ${t('settings.mcpAuthRequired') || 'Authentication required'}`);
   387	            });
   388	
   389	            // Fire authentication callback
   390	            if (onAuthRequired) {
   391	              onAuthRequired(server);
   392	            }
   393	            return;
   394	          }
   395	
   396	          if (truth.state === 'probed') {
   397	            // Persist the legacy `connected` value as probe-reachable and save
   398	            // the probe-reported inventory. Session receipts own chat readiness.
   399	            // On success, do not modify the enabled field - let the user decide whether to install
   400	            const successUpdate = await updateServerStatus('connected', {
   401	              tools: result.tools?.map((tool) => ({
   402	                name: tool.name,
   403	                description: tool.description,
   404	                ...(tool._meta ? { _meta: tool._meta } : {}),
   405	              })),
   406	              lastConnected: Date.now(),
   407	              lastError: undefined,
   408	            });
   409	            if (successUpdate.outcome !== 'applied') return;
   410	            await globalMessageQueue.add(() => {
   411	              message.success(
   412	                `${server.name}: ${t('settings.mcpProbeSuccess', 'Server probe succeeded; a new chat will verify its tools')}`
   413	              );
   414	            });
   415	
   416	            // Standalone probe succeeded; no session-readiness claim is made.
   417	          } else {
   418	            const errorMsg = truncateErrorMessage(result.error || t('settings.mcpError'));
   419	            await recordProbeFailure(errorMsg);
   420	          }
   421	        } else {
   422	          const errorMsg = truncateErrorMessage(response.msg || t('settings.mcpError'));
   423	          await recordProbeFailure(errorMsg);
   424	        }
   425	      } catch (error) {
   426	        const errorMsg = truncateErrorMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : t('settings.mcpError'));
   427	        await recordProbeFailure(errorMsg);
   428	      } finally {
   429	        setTestingServers((prev) => ({ ...prev, [server.id]: false }));
   430	      }
   431	    },
   432	    [saveMcpServers, message, t, onAuthRequired, removeMcpFromAgents, syncMcpToAgents, readMcpServers]
   433	  );
   434	
   435	  // Passive, non-destructive status refresh. Probes the given ENABLED servers
   436	  // concurrently to populate probe status + probe-reported tool counts, then
   437	  // writes all results
   438	  // in a SINGLE save. Unlike handleTestMcpConnection it does NOT toast and does
   439	  // NOT auto-disable a server on failure (a transient probe must never silently
   440	  // turn off the user's MCP). Servers in the legacy `connected`
   441	  // (probe-reachable) state and probed within
   442	  // STALE_MS are skipped unless `force` is set, so visiting the page does not
   443	  // re-spawn every stdio server on each render.
   444	  const refreshServerStatuses = useCallback(
   445	    async (servers: IMcpServer[], options?: { force?: boolean }) => {
   446	      const force = options?.force ?? false;
   447	      const STALE_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
   448	      const now = Date.now();
   449	      const targets = servers.filter(
   450	        (s) =>
   451	          s.enabled === true &&
   452	          !(s.status === 'error' && hasPublicationDivergence(s)) &&
   453	          (force || s.status !== 'connected' || typeof s.lastConnected !== 'number' || now - s.lastConnected > STALE_MS)
   454	      );
   455	      if (targets.length === 0) {
   456	        return;
   457	      }
   458	
   459	      setTestingServers((prev) => {
   460	        const next = { ...prev };
   461	        for (const s of targets) next[s.id] = true;
   462	        return next;
   463	      });
   464	
   465	      const updates = await Promise.all(
   466	        targets.map(async (server): Promise<{ id: string; expectedUpdatedAt: number; patch: Partial<IMcpServer> }> => {
   467	          try {
   468	            const response = await mcpService.testMcpConnection.invoke(server);
   469	            if (response.success && response.data) {
   470	              const result = response.data;
   471	              const truth = readCorrelatedMcpPrepublicationTruth(server, result);
   472	              if (truth.state === 'authentication-required') {
   473	                return {
   474	                  id: server.id,
   475	                  expectedUpdatedAt: server.updatedAt,
   476	                  patch: { status: 'disconnected', lastError: undefined },
   477	                };
   478	              }
   479	              if (truth.state === 'probed') {
   480	                return {
   481	                  id: server.id,
   482	                  expectedUpdatedAt: server.updatedAt,
   483	                  patch: {
   484	                    status: 'connected',
   485	                    tools: result.tools?.map((tool) => ({
   486	                      name: tool.name,
   487	                      description: tool.description,
   488	                      ...(tool._meta ? { _meta: tool._meta } : {}),
   489	                    })),
   490	                    lastConnected: Date.now(),
   491	                    lastError: undefined,
   492	                  },
   493	                };
   494	              }
   495	              // Probe failed: surface the error state but leave `enabled` alone.
   496	              return {
   497	                id: server.id,
   498	                expectedUpdatedAt: server.updatedAt,
   499	                patch: { status: 'error', lastError: truncateErrorMessage(result.error || t('settings.mcpError')) },
   500	              };
   501	            }
   502	            return {
   503	              id: server.id,
   504	              expectedUpdatedAt: server.updatedAt,
   505	              patch: { status: 'error', lastError: truncateErrorMessage(response.msg || t('settings.mcpError')) },
   506	            };
   507	          } catch (err) {
   508	            return {
   509	              id: server.id,
   510	              expectedUpdatedAt: server.updatedAt,
   511	              patch: {
   512	                status: 'error',
   513	                lastError: truncateErrorMessage(err instanceof Error ? err.message : t('settings.mcpError')),
   514	              },
   515	            };
   516	          }
   517	        })
   518	      );
   519	
   520	      try {
   521	        await saveMcpServers((prevServers) =>
   522	          prevServers.map((s) => {
   523	            const update = updates.find((u) => u.id === s.id);
   524	            return update && update.expectedUpdatedAt === s.updatedAt
   525	              ? { ...s, ...update.patch, updatedAt: nextMcpRevision(s.updatedAt) }
   526	              : s;
   527	          })
   528	        );
   529	      } finally {
   530	        setTestingServers((prev) => {
   531	          const next = { ...prev };
   532	          for (const s of targets) next[s.id] = false;
   533	          return next;
   534	        });
   535	      }
   536	    },
   537	    [saveMcpServers, t]
   538	  );
   539	
   540	  return {
   541	    testingServers,
   542	    handleTestMcpConnection,
   543	    refreshServerStatuses,
   544	  };
   545	};

    51	      const existingPublished = currentServers.find(
    52	        (server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey && server.enabled
    53	      );
    54	      let revocationAttempted = false;
    55	
    56	      const savedDeclaration: Omit<IMcpServer, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'> = {
    57	        ...serverData,
    58	        enabled: false,
    59	        status: 'disconnected' as const,
    60	        tools: undefined,
    61	        lastConnected: undefined,
    62	        lastError: undefined,
    63	      };
    64

    66	      try {
    67	        // Add/import is declaration persistence, never adapter publication. If

    69	        // publication first so storage cannot diverge from an agent config.
    70	        if (existingPublished) {
    71	          revocationAttempted = true;

    73	        }

    77	          if (!sameDeclarationRevision(expectedExisting, actualExisting)) {
    78	            throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while add was in progress');
    79	          }

    81	            (server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey
    82	          );
    83	
    84	          if (existingServerIndex !== -1) {
    85	            // If a server with the same name exists, update the existing one
    86	            const updatedServers = [...prevServers];

    88	              ...updatedServers[existingServerIndex],

    91	            };
    92	            serverToSync = updatedServers[existingServerIndex];
    93	            return updatedServers;

    95	          // If no server with the same name exists, add a new server

    98	            id: newMcpServerId(),

   100	            updatedAt: now,
   101	          };
   102	          serverToSync = newServer;
   103	          return [...prevServers, newServer];

   107	        // the prior definition to every target whenever revocation was attempted.

   110	            await syncMcpToAgents(existingPublished, true);

   113	            Object.assign(failure, { rollbackErrors: [rollbackError] });
