   131	  // Batch-import MCP servers
   132	  const handleBatchImportMcpServers = useCallback(
   133	    async (serversData: Omit<IMcpServer, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>[]) => {
   134	      const now = Date.now();
   135	      const addedServers: IMcpServer[] = [];
   136	
   137	      const incomingKeys = new Set(serversData.map((server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name)));
   138	      const currentServers = await readMcpServers();
   139	      const expectedByKey = new Map(
   140	        [...incomingKeys].map((key) => [
   141	          key,
   142	          currentServers.find((server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === key),
   143	        ])
   144	      );
   145	      const existingPublished = currentServers.filter(
   146	        (server) => server.enabled && incomingKeys.has(mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name))
   147	      );
   148	      const revocations = await Promise.allSettled(
   149	        existingPublished.map((server) => removeMcpFromAgents(server.name, undefined, server.transport.type))
   150	      );
   151	      const revocationFailures = revocations
   152	        .filter((result): result is PromiseRejectedResult => result.status === 'rejected')
   153	        .map((result) => result.reason);
   154	
   155	      const restoreRevoked = async (): Promise<unknown[]> => {
   156	        // Restore every prior definition, including operations reported as
   157	        // partial failures: rejected wrappers may already have changed agents.
   158	        const restorations = await Promise.allSettled(existingPublished.map((server) => syncMcpToAgents(server, true)));
   159	        return restorations
   160	          .filter((result): result is PromiseRejectedResult => result.status === 'rejected')
   161	          .map((result) => result.reason);
   162	      };
   163	
   164	      if (revocationFailures.length > 0) {
   165	        const restorationFailures = await restoreRevoked();
   166	        throw new AggregateError(
   167	          [...revocationFailures, ...restorationFailures],
   168	          'Failed to revoke existing MCP publications for import'
   169	        );
   170	      }
   171	
   172	      // Use functional update to avoid stale-closure issues
   173	      try {
   174	        await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
   175	          addedServers.length = 0;
   176	          const updatedServers = [...prevServers];
   177	
   178	          for (const incomingKey of incomingKeys) {
   179	            const actual = prevServers.find((server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey);
   180	            if (!sameDeclarationRevision(expectedByKey.get(incomingKey), actual)) {
   181	              throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while import was in progress');
   182	            }
   183	          }
   184	
   185	          serversData.forEach((serverData) => {
   186	            const savedDeclaration: Omit<IMcpServer, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'> = {
   187	              ...serverData,
   188	              enabled: false,
   189	              status: 'disconnected' as const,
   190	              tools: undefined,
   191	              lastConnected: undefined,
   192	              lastError: undefined,
   193	            };
   194	            const incomingKey = mcpServerCollisionKey(serverData.name);
   195	            const existingServerIndex = updatedServers.findIndex(
   196	              (server) => mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name) === incomingKey
   197	            );
   198	
   199	            if (existingServerIndex !== -1) {
   200	              // If a server with the same name exists, update the existing one
   201	              updatedServers[existingServerIndex] = {
   202	                ...updatedServers[existingServerIndex],
   203	                ...savedDeclaration,
   204	                updatedAt: nextMcpRevision(updatedServers[existingServerIndex].updatedAt),
   205	              };
   206	            } else {
   207	              // If no server with the same name exists, add a new server
   208	              const newServer: IMcpServer = {
   209	                ...savedDeclaration,
   210	                id: newMcpServerId(),
   211	                createdAt: now,
   212	                updatedAt: now,
   213	              };
   214	              updatedServers.push(newServer);
   215	              addedServers.push(newServer);
   216	            }
   217	          });
   218	
   219	          return updatedServers;
   220	        });
   221	      } catch (error) {
   222	        const restorationFailures = await restoreRevoked();
   223	        if (restorationFailures.length > 0) {
   224	          const failure = new Error('Failed to save MCP import and restore publication', { cause: error });
   225	          Object.assign(failure, { rollbackErrors: restorationFailures });

   273	  /**
   274	   * Save + connect a credential-bearing server: persist the installed server
   275	   * with the user's env-supplied credentials, run a REAL connection test that
   276	   * reaches the server and lists tools, and only enable the server when that
   277	   * test passes. Replaces the prior decorative token field whose value went
   278	   * nowhere and the false-positive "connected" banner.
   279	   *
   280	   * Shared by two auth models that connect identically — by starting the server
   281	   * with credentials already on it, NOT via the HTTP loopback OAuth path:
   282	   *  - api-key connectors (token embedded as a Bearer header by entryToServerData)
   283	   *  - stdio oauth2-byo connectors (#306): they carry their OAuth client creds on
   284	   *    transport.env (e.g. GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID/SECRET) and run their OWN OAuth
   285	   *    inside the spawned subprocess, so login() (which rejects non-HTTP
   286	   *    transports) must never be called for them.
   287	   */
   288	  const saveAndConnect = async () => {
   289	    const hasToken = Object.values(env).some((v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.trim().length > 0);
   290	    // Some api-key connectors take an OPTIONAL key (Context7's free tier works
   291	    // without one) - their guide has no token input. Only block on a missing
   292	    // token when the guide actually asks for one; otherwise connect keyless.
   293	    const requiresToken = !!guide?.steps?.some((s) => s.inputs && s.inputs.length > 0);
   294	    if (!hasToken && requiresToken) {
   295	      message.warning(t('mcpLibrary.install.tokenRequired', 'Enter your token first.'));
   296	      return;
   297	    }
   298	    setInstalling(true);
   299	    try {
   300	      const server = await crud.handleAddMcpServer(await buildServerData());
   301	      if (!server) {
   302	        message.error(t('mcpLibrary.install.errorFailed', 'Install failed: {{error}}', { error: 'unknown' }));
   303	        return;
   304	      }
   305	      const res = await mcpService.testMcpConnection.invoke(server);
   306	      let probeTruth: McpPrepublicationTruth | undefined;
   307	      if (res.success && res.data) {
   308	        probeTruth = readCorrelatedMcpPrepublicationTruth(server, res.data);
   309	      }
   310	      const ok = probeTruth?.state === 'probed';
   311	      if (!ok) {
   312	        const err = res.data?.error || res.msg || 'connection failed';
   313	        message.error(t('mcpLibrary.install.connectFailed', 'Could not connect: {{error}}', { error: err }));
   314	        if (server.enabled) await crud.handleToggleMcpServer(server.id, false).catch(() => {});
   315	        return;
   316	      }
   317	      let probedServer: IMcpServer | undefined;
   318	      await saveMcpServers((prev) => {
   319	        probedServer = undefined;
   320	        return prev.map((candidate) => {
   321	          if (candidate.id !== server.id || candidate.updatedAt !== server.updatedAt) return candidate;
   322	          const probedAt = Date.now();
   323	          probedServer = {
   324	            ...candidate,
   325	            status: 'connected',
   326	            tools: res.data?.tools?.map((tool) => ({
   327	              name: tool.name,
   328	              description: tool.description,
   329	              ...(tool._meta ? { _meta: tool._meta } : {}),
   330	            })),
   331	            lastConnected: probedAt,
   332	            lastError: undefined,
   333	            updatedAt: Math.max(probedAt, candidate.updatedAt + 1),
   334	          };
   335	          return probedServer;
   336	        });
   337	      });
   338	      if (!probedServer) return;
   339	      if (!probedServer.enabled) {
   340	        const published = await crud.handleToggleMcpServer(server.id, true, probedServer.updatedAt);
   341	        if (!published) return;
   342	      }
   343	      message.success(
   344	        t(
   345	          'mcpLibrary.install.probeSucceeded',
   346	          '{{name}} responded to the probe ({{count}} tools). New chats will verify availability.',
   347	          {
   348	            name: entry.title,
   349	            count: res.data?.tools?.length ?? 0,
   350	          }
   351	        )
   352	      );
   353	    } catch (err) {
   354	      message.error(
   355	        t('mcpLibrary.install.connectFailed', 'Could not connect: {{error}}', {
   356	          error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
   357	        })
   358	      );
   359	    } finally {
   360	      setInstalling(false);
   361	    }

   364	  const onPrimary = async (action: string) => {
   365	    // api-key hosted MCP: persist token + test + enable on success.
   370	    // Apple Ecosystem "Done - verify access" step. Full Disk Access is not
   371	    // queryable via API (macOS TCC) — the canonical verification is to spawn

   388	    // loopback OAuth path (login()) hard-rejects it ("OAuth requires an

   392	    if (transport === 'stdio') {

   398	    if (!server) {

   400	      if (!server) return;
   401	    }
   402	
   403	    const controller = new AbortController();
   404	    oauthAbortRef.current = controller;
   405	    const result = await login(server, oauthScopes, { signal: controller.signal });
   406	    oauthAbortRef.current = null;
   407	    if (result.success === true) {
   408	      await finishOAuthSuccess(server);
   409	      return;

   411	
   412	    // BYO short-circuit. The service-layer detected (or upstream errored

   414	    // credentials modal instead of surfacing a raw error.

   428	        error: (result.success === false && result.error) || 'unknown',

   158	  );

   160	  // Disable = disable + tear the config out of every agent (no live socket to
   161	  // close; agents reconnect lazily). Reconnect = enable + re-probe. Remove =
   162	  // delete from config + agents.
   163	  const disconnect = useCallback((serverId: string): void => void crud.handleToggleMcpServer(serverId, false), [crud]);
   164	  const reconnect = useCallback(
   165	    async (server: IMcpServer) => {
   166	      // Reconnect is an explicit reconciliation action, even when local truth
   167	      // remained enabled after an incomplete rollback. Republish first, then
   168	      // probe only the exact revision returned by that publication commit.
   169	      const publishedServer = await crud.handleToggleMcpServer(server.id, true);
   170	      if (!publishedServer) return;
   171	      await conn.handleTestMcpConnection(publishedServer);
   172	    },
   173	    [crud, conn]
   174	  );
   175	  const remove = useCallback((serverId: string): void => void crud.handleDeleteMcpServer(serverId), [crud]);
   176	  const removeStale = useCallback(
   177	    async (name: string) => {
   178	      await removeMcpFromAgents(name);
   179	      await computeStale();
   180	    },
   181	    [removeMcpFromAgents, computeStale]
   182	  );
   183	
   184	  return { rows, stale, refreshing, refresh, refreshMcpServers, disconnect, reconnect, remove, removeStale };

     1	/**
     2	 * @license
     3	 * Copyright 2026 Ferrox Labs
     4	 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     5	 */
     6	
     7	import type { McpOperationResult } from '../McpProtocol';
     8	import { AbstractMcpAgent } from '../McpProtocol';
     9	import type { IMcpServer } from '@/common/config/storage';
    10	import { ProcessConfig } from '@process/utils/initStorage';
    11	import { updateMcpConfig } from '../mcpConfigAuthority';
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * Wayland local MCP agent implementation
    15	 *
    16	 * Manages MCP configuration for the local Gemini CLI running via @office-ai/aioncli-core
    17	 *
    18	 * How it works:
    19	 * 1. MCP configuration is stored in ProcessConfig under 'mcp.config'
    20	 * 2. GeminiAgentManager reads from mcp.config at startup and converts it to @office-ai/aioncli-core format
    21	 * 3. @office-ai/aioncli-core uses these MCP servers at runtime
    22	 *
    23	 * Differences vs. other ACP Backend MCP Agents:
    24	 * - ACP Backend Agents: manage MCP configuration of real CLI tools (e.g. claude mcp, qwen mcp commands)
    25	 * - WaylandMcpAgent: manages runtime MCP configuration for Wayland's local @office-ai/aioncli-core
    26	 */
    27	export class WaylandMcpAgent extends AbstractMcpAgent {
    28	  constructor() {
    29	    // Use 'wayland' as the backend type to distinguish from the real Gemini CLI
    30	    // Even though config is consumed by GeminiAgentManager, at the MCP management layer it's a separate agent
    31	    super('wayland');
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  getSupportedTransports(): string[] {
    35	    // @office-ai/aioncli-core supports stdio, sse, http (streamable_http maps to http)
    36	    // Reference: node_modules/@office-ai/aioncli-core/dist/src/config/config.d.ts -> MCPServerConfig
    37	    return ['stdio', 'sse', 'http', 'streamable_http'];
    38	  }
    39	
    40	  /**
    41	   * Detect MCP configuration managed by Wayland
    42	   * Reads from the unified ProcessConfig
    43	   */
    44	  async detectMcpServers(_cliPath?: string): Promise<IMcpServer[]> {
    45	    try {
    46	      const mcpConfig = await ProcessConfig.get('mcp.config');
    47	      if (!mcpConfig || !Array.isArray(mcpConfig)) {
    48	        return [];
    49	      }
    50	
    51	      // Return all configured MCP servers
    52	      // Filter to those with transport types supported by @office-ai/aioncli-core
    53	      return mcpConfig.filter((server: IMcpServer) => {
    54	        const supportedTypes = this.getSupportedTransports();
    55	        return supportedTypes.includes(server.transport.type);
    56	      });
    57	    } catch (error) {
    58	      console.warn('[WaylandMcpAgent] Failed to detect MCP servers:', error);
    59	      return [];
    60	    }
    61	  }
    62	
    63	  /**
    64	   * Install MCP servers into the Wayland configuration
    65	   * In practice, merges the entries into the unified ProcessConfig
    66	   */
    67	  async installMcpServers(mcpServers: IMcpServer[]): Promise<McpOperationResult> {
    68	    try {
    69	      await updateMcpConfig((existingServers) => {
    70	        const serverMap = new Map<string, IMcpServer>();
    71	        existingServers.forEach((server) => serverMap.set(server.name, server));
    72	
    73	        mcpServers.forEach((server) => {
    74	          if (this.getSupportedTransports().includes(server.transport.type)) {
    75	            const previous = serverMap.get(server.name);
    76	            serverMap.set(server.name, {
    77	              ...server,
    78	              updatedAt: Math.max(Date.now(), (previous?.updatedAt ?? 0) + 1),
    79	            });
    80	          } else {
    81	            console.warn(
    82	              `[WaylandMcpAgent] Skipping ${server.name}: unsupported transport type ${server.transport.type}`
    83	            );
    84	          }
    85	        });
    86	        return Array.from(serverMap.values());
    87	      });
    88	
    89	      console.log('[WaylandMcpAgent] Installed MCP servers:', mcpServers.map((s) => s.name).join(', '));
    90	      return { success: true };
    91	    } catch (error) {
    92	      console.error('[WaylandMcpAgent] Failed to install MCP servers:', error);
    93	      return { success: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };
    94	    }
    95	  }
    96	
    97	  /**
    98	   * Remove an MCP server from the Wayland configuration
    99	   *
   100	   * Note: Wayland's MCP configuration is managed centrally by the renderer (frontend).
   101	   * This method is intentionally a no-op because:
   102	   * 1. When toggled off: the frontend already sets enabled: false; no backend mutation needed
   103	   * 2. When deleted: the frontend already removed it from config; no backend mutation needed
   104	   *
   105	   * WaylandMcpAgent is only responsible for reading config (detectMcpServers) and adding
   106	   * config (installMcpServers). It should not mutate config during remove to avoid
   107	   * conflicting with the frontend's configuration management.
   108	   */
   109	  removeMcpServer(mcpServerName: string): Promise<McpOperationResult> {
   110	    console.log(`[WaylandMcpAgent] Skip removing '${mcpServerName}' - config managed by renderer`);
   111	    return Promise.resolve({ success: true });
   112	  }
   113	}
     1	/**
     2	 * @license
     3	 * Copyright 2025 AionUi (aionui.com)
     4	 * Copyright 2026 Ferrox Labs
     5	 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     6	 * Modified by Ferrox Labs in 2026. Changes are documented in the project history.
     7	 */

     9	import { execSync } from 'child_process';
    10	import * as os from 'node:os';
    11	import type { IMcpServer } from '@/common/config/storage';
    12	import { ClaudeMcpAgent } from './agents/ClaudeMcpAgent';
    13	import { CodebuddyMcpAgent } from './agents/CodebuddyMcpAgent';
    14	import { QwenMcpAgent } from './agents/QwenMcpAgent';
    15	import { GeminiMcpAgent } from './agents/GeminiMcpAgent';
    16	import { WaylandMcpAgent } from './agents/WaylandMcpAgent';
    17	import { CodexMcpAgent } from './agents/CodexMcpAgent';
    18	import { OpencodeMcpAgent } from './agents/OpencodeMcpAgent';
    19	import { WCoreMcpAgent } from './agents/WCoreMcpAgent';
    20	import type { IMcpProtocol, DetectedMcpServer, McpConnectionTestResult, McpSyncResult, McpSource } from './McpProtocol';
    21	import { validateMcpServer, sanitizeMcpServerName } from './validateMcpServer';
    22	import { normalizeMcpServerForSpawn } from '@/common/mcp/normalizeMcpServer';
    23	import { mcpServerCollisionKey } from '@/common/mcp';
    24	import { bindMcpPrepublicationProbeTruth } from './mcpSessionTruthGate';
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * MCP service - coordinates the MCP operation protocol across agents
    28	 * New architecture: this layer only defines the protocol; implementations live in each Agent class
    29	 *
    30	 * Agent types:
    31	 * - AcpBackend ('claude', 'qwen', 'gemini', 'codex', etc.): supported ACP backends
    32	 * - 'wayland': @office-ai/aioncli-core (Wayland's locally managed Gemini implementation)
    33	 */
    34	export class McpService {
    35	  private agents: Map<McpSource, IMcpProtocol>;
    36	
    37	  /**
    38	   * Service-level operation lock to serialize heavy MCP operations.
    39	   * Prevents concurrent getAgentMcpConfigs / syncMcpToAgents / removeMcpFromAgents
    40	   * which would otherwise spawn dozens of child processes simultaneously,
    41	   * causing resource exhaustion and potential system freezes.
    42	   */
    43	  private operationQueue: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
    44	
    45	  private withServiceLock<T>(operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    46	    const queued = this.operationQueue.then(operation, () => operation());
    47	    // Keep the queue moving even if the operation rejects
    48	    this.operationQueue = queued.catch(() => {});
    49	    return queued;
    50	  }
    51	
    52	  private isCliAvailable(cliCommand: string): boolean {
    53	    const isWindows = process.platform === 'win32';
    54	    const whichCommand = isWindows ? 'where' : 'which';
    55	
    56	    // Keep original behavior: prefer where/which, then fallback on Windows to Get-Command.
    57	    try {
    58	      execSync(`${whichCommand} ${cliCommand}`, {
    59	        encoding: 'utf-8',
    60	        stdio: 'pipe',
    61	        timeout: 1000,
    62	      });
    63	      return true;
    64	    } catch {
    65	      if (!isWindows) return false;
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    if (isWindows) {
    69	      try {
    70	        // PowerShell fallback for shim scripts like *.ps1 (vfox)
    71	        execSync(
    72	          `powershell -NoProfile -NonInteractive -Command "Get-Command -All ${cliCommand} | Select-Object -First 1 | Out-Null"`,
    73	          {
    74	            encoding: 'utf-8',
    75	            stdio: 'pipe',
    76	            timeout: 1000,
    77	          }
    78	        );
    79	        return true;
    80	      } catch {
    81	        return false;
    82	      }
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    return false;
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  constructor() {
    89	    this.agents = new Map([
    90	      ['claude', new ClaudeMcpAgent()],
    91	      ['codebuddy', new CodebuddyMcpAgent()],
    92	      ['qwen', new QwenMcpAgent()],
    93	      ['gemini', new GeminiMcpAgent()],
    94	      ['wayland', new WaylandMcpAgent()], // Wayland local @office-ai/aioncli-core
    95	      ['codex', new CodexMcpAgent()],
    96	      ['opencode', new OpencodeMcpAgent()],
    97	      ['wcore', new WCoreMcpAgent()], // Wayland Core (Rust binary, TOML config)
    98	    ]);
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  /**
   102	   * Get the agent instance for a specific backend
   103	   */
   104	  private getAgent(backend: McpSource): IMcpProtocol | undefined {
   105	    return this.agents.get(backend);
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  /**
   109	   * Get the correct MCP agent instance based on agent config.
   110	   * Fork Gemini (cliPath=undefined) uses WaylandMcpAgent.
   111	   * Native Gemini (cliPath='gemini') uses GeminiMcpAgent.
   112	   */
   113	  private getAgentForConfig(agent: { backend: string; cliPath?: string }): IMcpProtocol | undefined {
   114	    // Fork Gemini uses WaylandMcpAgent to manage MCP config
   115	    if (agent.backend === 'gemini' && !agent.cliPath) {
   116	      return this.agents.get('wayland');
   117	    }
   118	    return this.agents.get(agent.backend as McpSource);
   119	  }
   120	
   121	  /**
   122	   * Ensure native Gemini CLI is in the agent list (if installed but not present).
   123	   * AcpDetector returns fork Gemini (cliPath=undefined), but MCP operations need native Gemini CLI too.
   124	   */
   125	  private addNativeGeminiIfNeeded(
   126	    agents: Array<{ backend: string; name: string; cliPath?: string }>
   127	  ): Array<{ backend: string; name: string; cliPath?: string }> {
   128	    const hasNativeGemini = agents.some((a) => a.backend === 'gemini' && a.cliPath === 'gemini');
   129	    if (hasNativeGemini) return agents;
   130	
   131	    try {
   132	      if (!this.isCliAvailable('gemini')) return agents;
   133	
   134	      const allAgents = [
   135	        ...agents,
   136	        {
   137	          backend: 'gemini',
   138	          name: 'Google Gemini CLI',
   139	          cliPath: 'gemini',
   140	        },
   141	      ];
   142	      console.log('[McpService] Added native Gemini CLI to agent list');
   143	      return allAgents;
   144	    } catch {
   145	      return agents;
   146	    }
   147	  }
   148	
   149	  /**
   150	   * Resolve which MCP agent should be used for config detection and how it
   151	   * should be reported back to the renderer.
   152	   */
   153	  private getDetectionTarget(agent: { backend: string; cliPath?: string }): {
   154	    agentInstance: IMcpProtocol | undefined;
   155	    source: McpSource;
   156	  } {
   157	    const agentInstance = this.getAgentForConfig(agent);
   158	    const source: McpSource = agent.backend === 'gemini' && !agent.cliPath ? 'gemini' : (agent.backend as McpSource);
   159	    return { agentInstance, source };
   160	  }
   161	
   162	  /**
   163	   * Merge detection results by source so the UI sees a single entry per agent.
   164	   * This also prevents duplicate Gemini rows when both built-in Gemini and the
   165	   * native Gemini CLI expose the same MCP server names.
   166	   */
   167	  private mergeDetectedServers(results: DetectedMcpServer[]): DetectedMcpServer[] {
   168	    const merged = new Map<McpSource, Map<string, IMcpServer>>();
   169	
   170	    results.forEach((result) => {
   171	      const serversByName = merged.get(result.source) ?? new Map<string, IMcpServer>();
   172	
   173	      result.servers.forEach((server) => {
   174	        const collisionKey = mcpServerCollisionKey(server.name);
   175	        if (!server.id || !collisionKey) throw new Error(`Invalid MCP detection result for source "${result.source}"`);
   176	        const existing = serversByName.get(collisionKey);
   177	        if (existing && JSON.stringify(existing) !== JSON.stringify(server)) {
   178	          throw new Error(
   179	            `Conflicting MCP detection results for source "${result.source}" and server "${server.name}"`
   180	          );
   181	        }
   182	        serversByName.set(collisionKey, existing ?? server);
   183	      });
   184	
   185	      merged.set(result.source, serversByName);
   186	    });
   187	
   188	    return Array.from(merged.entries()).map(([source, serversByName]) => ({
   189	      source,
   190	      servers: Array.from(serversByName.values()),
   191	    }));
   192	  }
   193	
   194	  /**
   195	   * Get MCP configuration from detected ACP agents (concurrent version)
   196	   *
   197	   * Note: this method also detects the native Gemini CLI's MCP configuration,
   198	   * even when it is disabled in ACP config (because fork Gemini is used for ACP).
   199	   */
   200	  getAgentMcpConfigs(
   201	    agents: Array<{
   202	      backend: string;
   203	      name: string;
   204	      cliPath?: string;
   205	    }>
   206	  ): Promise<DetectedMcpServer[]> {
   207	    return this.withServiceLock(async () => {
   208	      // Build the full detection list, including ACP agents plus the native Gemini CLI
   209	      const allAgentsToCheck = this.addNativeGeminiIfNeeded(agents);
   210	
   211	      // Run MCP detection across all agents concurrently
   212	      const promises = allAgentsToCheck.map(async (agent): Promise<DetectedMcpServer> => {
   213	        try {
   214	          const { agentInstance, source } = this.getDetectionTarget(agent);
   215	          if (!agentInstance) {
   216	            throw new Error(`MCP detection is not supported for backend "${agent.backend}"`);
   217	          }
   218	
   219	          const servers = await agentInstance.detectMcpServers(agent.cliPath);
   220	          console.log(
   221	            `[McpService] Detected ${servers.length} MCP servers for ${agent.backend} (cliPath: ${agent.cliPath || 'default'})`
   222	          );
   223	
   224	          return { source, servers };
   225	        } catch (error) {
   226	          console.warn(`[McpService] Failed to detect MCP servers for ${agent.backend}:`, error);
   227	          throw new Error(
   228	            `Incomplete MCP detection for backend "${agent.backend}": ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
   229	            { cause: error }
   230	          );
   231	        }
   232	      });
   233	
   234	      const results = await Promise.all(promises);
   235	      return this.mergeDetectedServers(results);
   236	    });
   237	  }
   238	
   239	  /**
   240	   * Get supported transport types for a given agent config.
   241	   * Fork Gemini (backend='gemini', no cliPath) uses WaylandMcpAgent.
   242	   */
   243	  getSupportedTransportsForAgent(agent: { backend: string; cliPath?: string }): string[] {
   244	    const agentInstance = this.getAgentForConfig(agent as { backend: string; cliPath?: string });
   245	    return agentInstance ? agentInstance.getSupportedTransports() : [];
   246	  }
   247	
   248	  /**
   249	   * Test connection to an MCP server
   250	   */
   251	  async testMcpConnection(server: IMcpServer): Promise<McpConnectionTestResult> {
   252	    // A probe is an operation on an exact saved declaration. Validate before
   253	    // normalization/spawn so malformed or unsafe declarations cannot acquire
   254	    // probe truth merely because an adapter happened to tolerate them.
   255	    validateMcpServer(server);
   256	    // Use the first available agent to test the connection; the test logic in the base class is generic
   257	    const firstAgent = this.agents.values().next().value;
   258	    if (firstAgent) {
   259	      // Translate a stored declaration into the transport that must actually be
   260	      // spawned (e.g. the filesystem server's allowed directories moved from the
   261	      // ineffective ALLOWED_DIRS env var to positional args, defaulting to home)
   262	      // BEFORE probing, so the Library "Needs attention" status reflects the real
   263	      // spawn instead of failing with "Connection closed" (#448).
   264	      const spawnable = normalizeMcpServerForSpawn(server, os.homedir());
   265	      // Reuse Wayland's stored OAuth bearer for the test, exactly like
   266	      // syncMcpToAgents does. Without it, an already-authorized hosted server
   267	      // (Notion/Canva/...) 401s here, reports needsAuth, and the stored status
   268	      // never advances to 'connected' - so the Library UI shows "Not connected"
   269	      // even though every agent CLI has the server connected.
   270	      const authedServer = await this.attachOAuthToken(spawnable);
   271	      const rawResult = await firstAgent.testMcpConnection(authedServer);
   272	      return bindMcpPrepublicationProbeTruth(server, rawResult);
   273	    }
   274	    return bindMcpPrepublicationProbeTruth(server, {
   275	      success: false,
   276	      error: 'No agent available for connection testing',
   277	    });
   278	  }

   280	  /**
   281	   * Sync MCP configuration to all detected agents
   282	   */
   283	  syncMcpToAgents(
   284	    mcpServers: IMcpServer[],
   285	    agents: Array<{
   286	      backend: string;
   287	      name: string;
   288	      cliPath?: string;
   289	    }>
   290	  ): Promise<McpSyncResult> {
   291	    // Only sync enabled MCP servers. Sanitize each name into the conservative
   292	    // identifier the agent CLIs require BEFORE validating - a stored server can
   293	    // carry a raw catalog id with a slash (e.g. "com.slack/slack-mcp") that
   294	    // older installs / JSON imports never sanitized, which would otherwise crash
   295	    // every sync. removeMcpFromAgents applies the same transform so the keys match.
   296	    const enabledServers = mcpServers
   297	      .filter((server) => server.enabled)
   298	      .map((server) =>
   299	        normalizeMcpServerForSpawn({ ...server, name: sanitizeMcpServerName(server.name) }, os.homedir())
   300	      );
   301	
   302	    if (enabledServers.length === 0) {
   303	      return Promise.resolve({
   304	        success: false,
   305	        results: [
   306	          { agent: 'mcp-service', success: false, error: 'No enabled MCP server was supplied for publication' },
   307	        ],
   308	      });
   309	    }
   310	
   311	    // Reject command-injection-prone names / non-http(s) URLs before any agent
   312	    // builds a CLI invocation from them (SEC-MCP-01 pre-sync guard).
   313	    for (const server of enabledServers) {
   314	      validateMcpServer(server);
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    return this.withServiceLock(async () => {
   318	      // Ensure native Gemini CLI is also in the sync list
   319	      const allAgents = this.addNativeGeminiIfNeeded(agents);
   320	      if (allAgents.length === 0) {
   321	        return {
   322	          success: false,
   323	          results: [{ agent: 'mcp-service', success: false, error: 'No MCP publication target is available' }],
   324	        };
   325	      }
   326	
   327	      // Attach the OAuth bearer for already-authorized servers so the agent
   328	      // CLIs reuse Wayland's token instead of starting their OWN ephemeral-port
   329	      // OAuth flow. That second flow's callback server is torn down before the
   330	      // user finishes authorizing in the browser, so the redirect hits a dead
   331	      // port (ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED) and the MCP never connects even though
   332	      // Wayland's own login succeeded. Injecting the token we already hold makes
   333	      // the engine skip its OAuth entirely.
   334	      const authedServers = await this.attachOAuthTokens(enabledServers);
   335	
   336	      // Run MCP sync across all agents concurrently
   337	      const promises = allAgents.map(async (agent) => {
   338	        try {
   339	          // Use getAgentForConfig to correctly distinguish fork Gemini from native Gemini
   340	          const agentInstance = this.getAgentForConfig(agent);
   341	          if (!agentInstance) {
   342	            console.warn(`[McpService] Skipping MCP sync for unsupported backend: ${agent.backend}`);
   343	            return {
   344	              agent: agent.name,
   345	              success: false,
   346	              error: `MCP publication is not supported for backend "${agent.backend}"`,
   347	            };
   348	          }
   349	
   350	          const result = await agentInstance.installMcpServers(authedServers);
   351	          return {
   352	            agent: agent.name,
   353	            success: result.success,
   354	            error: result.error,
   355	          };
   356	        } catch (error) {
   357	          return {
   358	            agent: agent.name,
   359	            success: false,
   360	            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
   361	          };
   362	        }
   363	      });
   364	
   365	      const results = await Promise.all(promises);
   366	
   367	      const allSuccess = results.length > 0 && results.every((r) => r.success);
   368	
   369	      return { success: allSuccess, results };
   370	    });
   371	  }
   372	
   373	  /**
   374	   * Attach the stored OAuth bearer to http/sse/streamable_http servers that
   375	   * Wayland has already authorized, so the agent CLIs reuse it instead of
   376	   * starting their own OAuth. Servers with no stored token, and servers that
   377	   * already carry an explicit Authorization header (BYO), are returned
   378	   * unchanged. The original server objects are never mutated.
   379	   */
   380	  /**
   381	   * Public: return the servers with each one's CURRENT (refreshed) OAuth bearer
   382	   * attached to its transport headers. Used by the ACP session builder to pass a
   383	   * live-tokened hosted MCP into `session/new`, so the chat connects with a fresh
   384	   * token instead of the stale one baked into a CLI/engine config at sync time.
   385	   */
   386	  attachOAuthTokens(servers: IMcpServer[]): Promise<IMcpServer[]> {
   387	    return Promise.all(servers.map((server) => this.attachOAuthToken(server)));
   388	  }
   389	
   390	  /**
   391	   * Single-server variant of {@link attachOAuthTokens}. Returns the server with
   392	   * the stored OAuth bearer attached when one is held and no explicit
   393	   * Authorization header is already present; otherwise returns it unchanged.
   394	   * Never mutates the input.
   395	   */
   396	  private async attachOAuthToken(server: IMcpServer): Promise<IMcpServer> {
   397	    const transport = server.transport;
   398	    if (transport.type !== 'http' && transport.type !== 'sse' && transport.type !== 'streamable_http') {
   399	      return server;
   400	    }
   401	    const headers = transport.headers ?? {};
   402	    // Prefer a FRESH OAuth token from Wayland's token store over any Authorization
   403	    // header already baked into the record. The connect flow persists the bearer
   404	    // into the server's transport; once it expires, leaving it in place both masks
   405	    // the refresh (we'd skip getValidToken) and keeps sending the dead token, which
   406	    // the server rejects as invalid_token - the endless "sign in again" loop. Since
   407	    // getValidToken refreshes an expired token, this keeps the bearer current. Only
   408	    // when there is NO stored OAuth token do we respect a user-supplied (BYO)
   409	    // Authorization header as-is. The original server object is never mutated.
   410	    //
   411	    // Import McpOAuthService dynamically (not at module top level): it pulls the
   412	    // aioncli-core OAuth chain whose OAuthCredentialStorage static initializer
   413	    // references HybridTokenStorage, and a top-level import triggers a
   414	    // module-init TDZ ("Cannot access 'HybridTokenStorage' before initialization")
   415	    // in any module that imports McpService. Deferring the import past the
   416	    // non-http early return keeps the chain out of module load.
   417	    const { mcpOAuthService } = await import('./McpOAuthService');
   418	    const token = await mcpOAuthService.getValidToken(server).catch((): string | null => null);
   419	    if (!token) {
   420	      return server;
   421	    }
   422	    const nonAuthHeaders = Object.fromEntries(
   423	      Object.entries(headers).filter(([k]) => k.toLowerCase() !== 'authorization')
   424	    );
   425	    return {
   426	      ...server,
   427	      transport: { ...transport, headers: { ...nonAuthHeaders, Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
   428	    };
   429	  }
   430	
   431	  /**
   432	   * Remove MCP configuration from all detected agents
   433	   */
   434	  removeMcpFromAgents(
   435	    mcpServerName: string,
   436	    agents: Array<{
   437	      backend: string;
   438	      name: string;
   439	      cliPath?: string;
   440	    }>
   441	  ): Promise<McpSyncResult> {
   442	    // Match the key syncMcpToAgents wrote so a server installed under a
   443	    // sanitized name (e.g. com.slack/slack-mcp -> com.slack-slack-mcp) is
   444	    // actually found and removed from each agent config.
   445	    const safeName = sanitizeMcpServerName(mcpServerName);
   446	    return this.withServiceLock(async () => {
   447	      // Ensure native Gemini CLI is also in the removal list
   448	      const allAgents = this.addNativeGeminiIfNeeded(agents);
   449	
   450	      // Run MCP removal across all agents concurrently
   451	      const promises = allAgents.map(async (agent) => {
   452	        try {
   453	          // Use getAgentForConfig to correctly distinguish fork Gemini from native Gemini
   454	          const agentInstance = this.getAgentForConfig(agent);
   455	          if (!agentInstance) {
   455	          if (!agentInstance) {
   456	            console.warn(`[McpService] Skipping MCP removal for unsupported backend: ${agent.backend}`);
   457	            return {
   458	              agent: `${agent.backend}:${agent.name}`,
   459	              success: false,
   460	              error: `MCP removal is not supported for backend "${agent.backend}"`,
   461	            };
   462	          }
   463	
   464	          const result = await agentInstance.removeMcpServer(safeName);
   465	          return {
   466	            agent: `${agent.backend}:${agent.name}`,
   467	            success: result.success,
   468	            error: result.error,
   469	          };
   470	        } catch (error) {
   471	          return {
   472	            agent: `${agent.backend}:${agent.name}`,
   473	            success: false,
   474	            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
   475	          };
   476	        }
   477	      });
   478	
   479	      const results = await Promise.all(promises);
   480	
   481	      // S12: mirror the sync path (syncMcpToAgents) - a per-agent removal that
   482	      // failed is captured in results[] with success:false, but returning a
   483	      // hardcoded `success: true` hid it, so the renderer reported "deleted"
   484	      // while the server stayed in that agent's CLI config (Claude/Codex/wcore
   485	      // drift). Reflect overall success from the per-agent results.
   486	      return { success: results.every((r) => r.success), results };
   487	    });
   488	  }

   189	  ipcBridge.mcpService.checkOAuthStatus.provider(async (server) => {
   190	    try {
   120	  if (Buffer.byteLength(raw, 'utf8') > MAX_ARCHIVE_BYTES) throw new Error('MCP connector archive is too large');
   121	  const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
   122	  assertPlainRecord(parsed, 'MCP connector archive');
   123	  assertExactKeys(
   124	    parsed,
   125	    ['kind', 'version', 'archiveId', 'archivedAt', 'serverDigest', 'server'],
   126	    'MCP connector archive'
   127	  );
   128	  if (
   129	    parsed.kind !== ARCHIVE_KIND ||
   130	    parsed.version !== ARCHIVE_VERSION ||
   131	    typeof parsed.archiveId !== 'string' ||
   132	    typeof parsed.archivedAt !== 'number' ||
   133	    !Number.isFinite(parsed.archivedAt) ||
   134	    typeof parsed.serverDigest !== 'string' ||
   135	    !/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(parsed.serverDigest)
   136	  ) {
   137	    throw new Error('Invalid MCP connector archive metadata');
   138	  }
   139	  assertSafeArchiveId(parsed.archiveId);
   140	  assertMcpServer(parsed.server);
   141	  if (digestMcpServer(parsed.server) !== parsed.serverDigest) {
   142	    throw new Error('MCP connector archive digest mismatch');
   143	  }
   144	  return parsed as unknown as McpConnectorArchiveRecord;
   145	}

   147	function toSummary(record: McpConnectorArchiveRecord): ArchivedMcpConnectorSummary {

   159	async function assertDirectoryNotSymlink(directory: string): Promise<void> {
   160	  const stat = await fs.lstat(directory);
   161	  if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error(`Unsafe MCP archive directory: ${directory}`);
   162	}
   163	
   164	async function ensurePrivateChildDirectory(parent: string, child: string): Promise<string> {
   165	  await assertDirectoryNotSymlink(parent);
   166	  const target = path.join(parent, child);
   167	  try {
   168	    await fs.mkdir(target, { mode: 0o700 });
   169	  } catch (error) {
   170	    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'EEXIST') throw error;
   171	  }
   172	  await assertDirectoryNotSymlink(target);
   173	  return target;
   174	}
   175	
   176	async function syncDirectory(directory: string): Promise<void> {
   177	  if (process.platform === 'win32') return;
   178	  const handle = await fs.open(directory, fsConstants.O_RDONLY);
   179	  try {
   180	    await handle.sync();
   181	  } finally {
   182	    await handle.close();
   183	  }
   184	}
   185	
   186	export class McpConnectorArchiveStore {
   187	  constructor(private readonly configRoot: string) {}
   188	
   189	  private async ensureRoot(): Promise<{ active: string; restored: string; aborted: string }> {
   190	    // Desktop deliberately exposes its config directory through a CLI-safe
   191	    // top-level symlink on macOS. Resolve that trusted root alias once, then
   192	    // reject every symlink below it so an `archives`/connector child cannot
   193	    // redirect secret-bearing records outside the real config directory.
   194	    const configRoot = await fs.realpath(this.configRoot);
   195	    await assertDirectoryNotSymlink(configRoot);
   196	    const archives = await ensurePrivateChildDirectory(configRoot, 'archives');
   197	    const mcp = await ensurePrivateChildDirectory(archives, 'mcp-connectors');
   198	    return {
   199	      active: await ensurePrivateChildDirectory(mcp, 'active'),
   200	      restored: await ensurePrivateChildDirectory(mcp, 'restored'),
   201	      aborted: await ensurePrivateChildDirectory(mcp, 'aborted'),
   202	    };
   203	  }
   204	
   205	  async publish(server: IMcpServer): Promise<McpConnectorArchiveRecord> {
   206	    assertMcpServer(server);
   207	    const { active } = await this.ensureRoot();
   208	    const archiveId = randomUUID();
   209	    const record: McpConnectorArchiveRecord = {
   210	      kind: ARCHIVE_KIND,
   211	      version: ARCHIVE_VERSION,
   212	      archiveId,
   213	      archivedAt: Date.now(),
   214	      serverDigest: digestMcpServer(server),
   215	      server: structuredClone(server),
   216	    };
   217	    const target = path.join(active, `${archiveId}.json`);
   218	    const temporary = path.join(active, `.${archiveId}.tmp`);
   219	    const handle = await fs.open(temporary, 'wx', 0o600);
   220	    let published = false;
   221	    try {
   222	      await handle.writeFile(`${JSON.stringify(record, null, 2)}\n`, 'utf8');
   223	      await handle.sync();
   224	      await handle.close();
   225	      const verified = parseArchiveRecord(await fs.readFile(temporary, 'utf8'));
   226	      if (verified.serverDigest !== record.serverDigest) throw new Error('MCP connector archive verification failed');
   227	      await fs.rename(temporary, target);
   228	      await syncDirectory(active);
   229	      published = true;
   230	      return record;
   231	    } finally {
   232	      await handle.close().catch(() => {});
   233	      if (!published) await fs.rm(temporary, { force: true }).catch(() => {});
   234	    }
   235	  }
   236	
   237	  async read(archiveId: string): Promise<McpConnectorArchiveRecord> {
   238	    assertSafeArchiveId(archiveId);
   239	    const { active } = await this.ensureRoot();
   240	    const filePath = path.join(active, `${archiveId}.json`);
   241	    const stat = await fs.lstat(filePath);
   242	    if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.size > MAX_ARCHIVE_BYTES) {
   243	      throw new Error('Unsafe MCP connector archive file');
   244	    }
   245	    const record = parseArchiveRecord(await fs.readFile(filePath, 'utf8'));
   246	    if (record.archiveId !== archiveId) throw new Error('MCP connector archive identity mismatch');
   247	    return record;
   248	  }
   249	
   250	  async list(): Promise<McpConnectorArchiveRecord[]> {
   251	    const { active } = await this.ensureRoot();
   252	    const entries = await fs.readdir(active, { withFileTypes: true });
   253	    // Inventory is an evidence surface. One corrupt/tampered record must not
   254	    // be silently omitted and translated into a deceptive "no archives"
   255	    // state; fail the whole list if any selected record fails validation.
   256	    const records = await Promise.all(
   257	      entries
   258	        .filter((entry) => entry.isFile() && !entry.isSymbolicLink() && entry.name.endsWith('.json'))
   259	        .map((entry) => this.read(entry.name.slice(0, -'.json'.length)))
   260	    );
   261	    return records.toSorted((left, right) => right.archivedAt - left.archivedAt);
   262	  }
   263	
   264	  async retire(archiveId: string, destination: 'restored' | 'aborted'): Promise<void> {
   265	    assertSafeArchiveId(archiveId);
   266	    const roots = await this.ensureRoot();
   267	    const source = path.join(roots.active, `${archiveId}.json`);
   268	    const target = path.join(roots[destination], `${archiveId}.json`);
   269	    try {
   270	      await fs.rename(source, target);
   271	      await syncDirectory(roots.active);
   272	      await syncDirectory(roots[destination]);
   273	    } catch (error) {
   274	      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
   275	      const targetStat = await fs.lstat(target).catch((_error: unknown): null => null);
   276	      if (!targetStat?.isFile() || targetStat.isSymbolicLink()) throw error;
   277	    }
   278	  }
   279	}
   280

   282	  private mutationTail: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
   283

   285	    private readonly archives: McpConnectorArchiveStore,
   286	    private readonly deps: McpConnectorLifecycleDependencies
   287	  ) {}
   288	
   289	  private serialize<T>(operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
   290	    const queued = this.mutationTail.then(operation, operation);

    18	// From this file (src/renderer/pages/settings/McpLibrary/hooks/) the catalog lives
    19	// 4 levels up at src/renderer/mcp-catalog/.
    20	const entryModules = import.meta.glob<{ default: CatalogEntry } | CatalogEntry>(
    21	  '@renderer/mcp-catalog/entries/*.json',
    22	  { eager: true },
    23	);
    24	const guideModules = import.meta.glob<string>(
    25	  '@renderer/mcp-catalog/guides/*.md',
    26	  { eager: true, query: '?raw', import: 'default' },
    27	);
    28	
    29	// Vite resolves SVG asset URLs at build time. Catalog stores `iconUrl` as a
    30	// literal string like "icons/google.svg" (the path inside the catalog dir),
    31	// so we map filename → Vite-bundled URL once and resolve at hook construction.
    32	const iconModules = import.meta.glob<string>(
    33	  '@renderer/mcp-catalog/icons/*.svg',
    34	  { eager: true, query: '?url', import: 'default' },
    35	);
    36	
    37	const iconUrlByFilename: Record<string, string> = (() => {
    38	  const map: Record<string, string> = {};
    39	  for (const [key, url] of Object.entries(iconModules)) {
    40	    const slash = key.lastIndexOf('/');
    41	    const filename = slash >= 0 ? key.slice(slash + 1) : key;
    42	    map[filename] = url;
    43	  }
    44	  return map;
    45	})();
    46	
    47	function resolveIconUrl(catalogIconUrl: string | undefined): string | undefined {
    48	  if (!catalogIconUrl) return undefined;
    49	  // Already absolute (Vite-resolved on a previous pass, or http(s))
    50	  if (catalogIconUrl.startsWith('/') || /^https?:\/\//i.test(catalogIconUrl)) {
    51	    return catalogIconUrl;
    52	  }
    53	  const filename = catalogIconUrl.replace(/^icons\//, '');
    54	  return iconUrlByFilename[filename];
    55	}
    56	
    57	// Strict schema for guide frontmatter - never trust unvalidated YAML.
    58	const SetupStepSchema = z.object({
    59	  id: z.string(),
    60	  title: z.string(),
    61	  estSeconds: z.coerce.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
    62	  autoCompletedByInstall: z.coerce.boolean().optional(),
    63	  externalAction: z.object({ label: z.string(), url: z.string().url() }).optional(),
    64	  primaryAction: z.object({ label: z.string(), action: z.string() }).optional(),
    65	  inputs: z
    66	    .array(
    67	      z.object({
    68	        name: z.string().regex(/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*$/),
    69	        label: z.string(),
    70	        placeholder: z.string().optional(),
    71	        secret: z.coerce.boolean().optional(),
    72	      }),
    73	    )
    74	    .optional(),
    75	  warning: z.string().optional(),
    76	  body: z.string().optional(),
    77	});
    78	
    79	const GuideFrontmatterSchema = z.object({
    80	  guideVersion: z.string(),
    81	  estimatedMinutes: z.coerce.number().int().positive(),
    82	  steps: z.array(SetupStepSchema).min(1).max(10),
    83	});

e3303e5cc39aefda83299a87e09cdc233f945eaa fix(mcp): make Claude Code publication and rollback idempotent
     1	# Plan — TVControl full integration in Wayland Desktop (2026-08-04)
     2	
     3	**Repo split:** TVControl is DONE (2.2.1 published, tagged `v2.2.1`, verified by clean
     4	install from the public registry: 101 tools over stdio). Every packet below is in
     5	**Wayland Desktop**, worktree `~/dev/wayland-worktrees/packet-attribution`.
     6	
     7	**Standing constraints:** no push / merge / tag without Sean. Never commit
     8	`src/process/services/constitution/constitutionFsAuthority.generated.ts`. `AGENTS.md` and
     9	`CLAUDE.md` are dirty from IJFW tooling — not ours, keep them out of every commit.
    10	76 commits currently local, nothing pushed.
    11	
    12	---
    13	
    14	## W-1 — Publication compare-and-set failure  🔴 BLOCKER
    15	
    16	Everything else is cosmetic next to this. It is **not** TVControl-specific: it makes
    17	enabling or reconnecting **any** connector unreliable, and a failure leaves the row in a
    18	state the UI presents as unrecoverable.
    19	
    20	### Observed behaviour (reproduced, logs in `/tmp/wl-run4.log`)
    21	
    22	Reconnect on a disabled stdio connector:
    23	
    24	```
    25	09:49:15.863 [WCoreMcpAgent]   Added MCP server: tvcontrol
    26	09:49:15.912 [WaylandMcpAgent] Installed MCP servers: tvcontrol
    27	09:49:16.852 [GeminiMcpAgent]  Added MCP server: tvcontrol
    28	09:49:34.264 [CodexMcpAgent]   Added MCP server: tvcontrol
    29	09:49:36.311 [ClaudeMcpAgent]  Added MCP server: tvcontrol     <- all five succeed
    30	09:50:22.078 ...               Removed MCP server: tvcontrol   <- rollback, 46s later
    31	```
    32	
    33	Publication succeeds everywhere, then the local commit fails and the rollback strips it
    34	back out. Durable result: `status: error`, `lastError: "publication rollback incomplete —
    35	reconnect this connector"`. Survives an app restart.
    36	
    37	### ⚠️ Root cause is NOT established — do not skip to a fix
    38	
    39	An earlier session attributed this to `refreshServerStatuses` racing the publication.
    40	**That attribution is unproven and probably wrong**: its target filter
    41	(`useMcpConnection.ts:459-466`) requires `s.enabled === true`, and the connector stays
    42	`enabled: false` in durable storage for the whole publication window, because
    43	`handleToggleMcpServer` publishes *before* it commits. It was never a candidate.
    44	
    45	Two hypotheses remain. They need different fixes, so identify which one first.
    46	
    47	**H1 — row-level guard.** `useMcpServerCRUD.ts:377-385`:
    48	
    49	```js
    50	await saveMcpServers((prevServers) => {
    51	  committed = false;
    52	  return prevServers.map((server) => {
    53	    if (server.id !== serverId || server.updatedAt !== targetServer.updatedAt) return server;
    54	    committed = true;
    55	    return updatedTargetServer;
    56	  });
    57	});
    58	if (!committed) throw new Error('MCP declaration changed while publication was in progress');
    59	```
    60	
    61	`targetServer.updatedAt` is read at t0 and compared at t0+45s. Any writer that bumps that
    62	row's `updatedAt` in between makes `committed` false. Full writer list is 16 call sites —
    63	`rg 'saveMcpServers\(' src/renderer`.
    64	
    65	**H2 — global revision exhaustion.** `enqueueMcpConfigWrite`
    66	(`useMcpServers.ts:35-61`) CASes on a **whole-config** `revision`, so a write to *any*
    67	server invalidates a write to *every* server. It retries `MAX_MCP_CONFIG_CAS_ATTEMPTS = 16`
    68	then throws `MCP config changed too frequently to commit safely` — which lands in the same
    69	catch and produces the same rollback. Agent-detection sweeps run every few seconds during
    70	publication, so sustained revision churn is plausible.
    71	
    72	H1 and H2 are distinguishable by the thrown message alone.
    73	
    74	### Phase A — diagnose (do this first, it is cheap)
    75	
    76	1. Log every `enqueueMcpConfigWrite` entry: caller tag, attempt number, whether the CAS
    77	   applied, and the target row's `updatedAt` before and after.
    78	2. Reproduce: clean dev profile, add an stdio connector, click Reconnect, capture the log.
    79	   Reproduction recipe is in `.planning/HANDOFF-2026-08-04-tvcontrol.md`.
    80	3. **Deliverable: the named writer and the exact thrown message.** No fix lands before this.
    81	
    82	Reproduction is reliable — it fired on every reconnect attempt across three runs, because
    83	five serialized CLI agents take 20-45s to publish.
    84	
    85	### Phase B — fix, per finding
    86	
    87	- **If H1:** stop comparing a 45-second-old `updatedAt`. Re-read the row inside the updater
    88	  and accept the commit when the declaration is materially unchanged, or move the row guard
    89	  onto an identity that publication owns rather than one every writer bumps.
    90	- **If H2:** the retry budget is not the fix — raise it and it still fails under load.
    91	  Either scope the CAS to the row rather than the whole config, or serialize
    92	  user-initiated publication against background writers.
    93	
    94	Whichever it is, a **failed local commit after a fully successful publication must not be
    95	reported as a rollback failure.** Publication succeeded; the correct recovery is to keep
    96	it and re-commit, not to strip five agents and mark the row unrecoverable.
    97	
    98	### Acceptance
    99	
   100	- Enabling a connector whose publication takes >30s commits and does not roll back.
   101	- Regression test in `tests/unit/useMcpServerCRUD.dom.test.tsx` (71 assertions already
   102	  there): a slow `syncMcpToAgents` with a competing writer underneath it must still commit.
   103	  **The test must fail against current `main`** — prove it before fixing.
   104	- After a genuine publication failure, the row returns to a state the user can retry from.
   105	
   106	---
   107	
   108	## W-2 — Land the two Claude Code agent fixes  (already built)
   109	
   110	Committed locally as `e3303e5cc`, needs a PR. Independent of W-1, can run in parallel.
   111	
   112	Both reproduced against the real CLI, both fixed in
   113	`src/process/services/mcpServices/agents/ClaudeMcpAgent.ts`:
   114	
   115	- `claude mcp add-json` is not an upsert — exits 1 with `MCP server X already exists in
   116	  user config`. Only the **stdio** path uses `add-json`; the HTTP/SSE path uses `claude mcp
   117	  add`, which overwrites. So re-publishing any stdio connector to Claude Code always failed
   118	  while the same operation on a remote connector always succeeded. Now removes user-scope
   119	  then re-adds.
   120	- `claude mcp remove` on an absent server exits 1 with `No MCP server named "X" in <scope>
   121	  scope`. The absence check looked for `not found` / `does not exist` **and read
   122	  `error.message`, which `safeExecFile` fixes to `Command failed with exit code 1`** — so no
   123	  wording could ever have matched and rollback always "failed". Now classifies on
   124	  `execErrorDetail`, which carries stderr. `CodexMcpAgent` already did both.
   125	
   126	Tests in `tests/unit/claudeMcpAgent.test.ts` assert against the CLI's verbatim stderr for
   127	all three removal scopes, plus a negative control (permission denied, timeout) so neither
   128	predicate can degrade into `() => true`. 7 pass; typecheck clean; 65 MCP unit tests and
   129	71 CRUD DOM tests pass.
   130	
   131	Verified live: a connector stuck in the divergent state recovers on reconnect, logging
   132	`[ClaudeMcpAgent] Replaced existing MCP server: tvcontrol`.
   133	
   134	---
   135	
   136	## W-3 — TVControl as a first-class catalog connector
   137	
   138	Do **after** W-1. Installing from the catalog runs the same publication path, so shipping
   139	this first just gives people a broken first-run.
   140	
   141	Four files, no new machinery:
   142	
   143	- `src/renderer/mcp-catalog/entries/com.ferroxlabs-tvcontrol.json` — copy the shape of
   144	  `entries/ai.perplexity-perplexity-mcp.json`: `registryType: "npm"`,
   145	  `identifier: "@ferroxlabs/tvcontrol"`, `version: "latest"`, `runtimeHint: "npx"`,
   146	  `transport: { type: "stdio" }`, no `environmentVariables` (TVControl needs none).
   147	  `x-wayland`: `license: "MIT"`, `auth.method: "local-credentials"`,
   148	  `platforms: ["macos","windows"]`, categories/tags for trading.
   149	- a row in `src/renderer/mcp-catalog/catalog.json` (107 entries today).
   150	- `src/renderer/mcp-catalog/icons/com.ferroxlabs-tvcontrol.svg`.
   151	- `src/renderer/mcp-catalog/guides/com.ferroxlabs-tvcontrol.md` — **this carries the
   152	  precondition**: TradingView Desktop must be running with a remote debugging port.
   153	  `scripts/launch_tv_debug_mac.sh` / `launch_tv_debug.bat` in the TVControl package do it,
   154	  and the Windows script already handles the MSIX/Store install via `Get-AppxPackage`.
   155	
   156	Verified plumbing: `entryToServerData.ts:121` maps that entry to `command: 'npx'`, and
   157	`resolveMcpStdioSpawn` (`mcpStdioSpawn.ts:38-42`) rewrites `npx` to Wayland's bundled Bun —
   158	so it installs and runs on a machine with no system node. The catalog is glob-imported by
   159	`useMcpLibrary.ts:21-33`; no separate build step, `bun run package` picks it up.
   160	
   161	**Do NOT bundle it as a builtin** and do not build detection-gating. An earlier
   162	recommendation said detection-gate it; that reasoning was about shipping it as an
   163	always-on builtin, where most users would get a dead tool. A catalog entry is opt-in — the
   164	user clicks Install — so the concern is moot. The catalog schema has no precondition
   165	concept (`x-wayland` supports only `platforms` and `minWaylandVersion`), adding one is a
   166	feature in its own right, and `chrome-devtools`, `playwright` and `apple-mcp` all ship with
   167	host preconditions handled by their setup guide. Be consistent with them.
   168	
   169	**Do not flip `TV_MCP_ADVANCED`.** `ui_evaluate` executes arbitrary page JavaScript and is
   170	unregistered by default. The catalog entry must not set it.
   171	
   172	---
   173	
   174	## Sequencing
   175	
   176	```
   177	W-1 Phase A (diagnose)  ─┐
   178	                         ├─> W-1 Phase B (fix) ──> W-3 (catalog entry)
   179	W-2 (PR the agent fixes) ─┘   [independent, parallel]
   180	```

   184	## Optional TVControl follow-ups (separate repo, not blocking)
   185	
   186	1. **The "offline" suite is not offline.** `tests/state.test.js` connects to a live
   187	   TradingView whenever one is listening on 9222 — it ran for 11 minutes against Sean's
   188	   real chart before being killed. An ordinary `npm test` can mutate a live chart. Same
   189	   shape as the bug just fixed: the tests did not know what they were talking to.
   190	2. **Two moderate advisories remain** in the SDK's HTTP stack (`hono`,
   191	   `@hono/node-server`, `body-parser`). Below the CI threshold and unreachable — TVControl
   192	   only runs stdio. Clearing them means SDK 1.30.0, which deserves its own PR with
   193	   `tests/mcp_stdio.test.js` as the gate, not a drive-by bump. That test is the only thing
   194	   standing between an SDK bump and another silent total outage.

    11	/**
    12	 * Truncate long error messages to keep them readable
    13	 */
    14	const truncateErrorMessage = (message: string, maxLength: number = 150): string => {
    15	  if (message.length <= maxLength) {
    16	    return message;
    17	  }
    18	  return message.substring(0, maxLength) + '...';
    19	};

    26	const progressIcon = () =>

    48	export const useMcpOperations = (

    51	) => {
    52	  const { t } = useTranslation();
    53	
    54	  // Handle results of syncing MCP config to agents
    55	  const handleMcpOperationResult = useCallback(
    56	    async (
    57	      response: McpOperationResponse,
    58	      operation: 'sync' | 'remove',
    59	      successMessage?: string,
    60	      skipRecheck = false
    61	    ) => {
    62	      if (response.success && response.data) {
    63	        const { results } = response.data;
    64	        const failedAgents = results.filter((r: McpOperationResult) => !r.success);
    65	
    66	        // Show operation-start message immediately, then trigger state update
    67	        if (failedAgents.length > 0) {
    68	          const failedNames = failedAgents
    69	            .map((r: McpOperationResult) => `${r.agent}: ${truncateErrorMessage(r.error || '')}`)
    70	            .join(', ');
    71	          const truncatedErrors = truncateErrorMessage(failedNames, 200);
    72	          const partialFailedKey = operation === 'sync' ? 'mcpSyncPartialFailed' : 'mcpRemovePartialFailed';
    73	          await globalMessageQueue.add(() => {
    74	            message.warning({
    75	              content: t(`settings.${partialFailedKey}`, { errors: truncatedErrors }),
    76	              duration: 6000,
    77	            });
    78	          });
    79	        } else {
    80	          if (successMessage) {
    81	            await globalMessageQueue.add(() => {
    82	              message.success(successMessage);
    83	            });
    84	          }
    85	          // No longer show the "operation started" message; it was already shown at the start
    86	        }
    87	
    88	        // Then update UI state
    89	        if (!skipRecheck) {
    90	          void ConfigStorage.get('mcp.config')
    91	            .then((latestServers) => {
    92	              if (latestServers) {
    93	                // A status check can be triggered here, but callers must supply a callback
    94	              }
    95	            })
    96	            .catch(() => {
    97	              // Handle loading error silently
    98	            });
    99	        }
   100	      } else {
   101	        const failedKey = operation === 'sync' ? 'mcpSyncFailed' : 'mcpRemoveFailed';
   102	        const errorMsg = truncateErrorMessage(response.msg || t('settings.unknownError'));
   103	        await globalMessageQueue.add(() => {
   104	          message.error({ content: t(`settings.${failedKey}`, { error: errorMsg }), duration: 6000 });
   105	        });
   106	      }
   107	    },
   108	    [message, t]
   109	  );
   110	
   111	  // Remove MCP config from agents
   112	  const removeMcpFromAgents = useCallback(

   114	      const agentsResponse = await acpConversation.getAvailableAgents.invoke();
   115	      if (agentsResponse.success && agentsResponse.data) {
   116	        // Filter agents by transport type support if transport type is known
   117	        const compatibleCount = transportType

   119	          : agentsResponse.data.length;
   120

   123	          message.info({ content: t('settings.mcpRemoveStarted', { count: compatibleCount }), icon: progressIcon() });
   124	        });

   127	        // HTTP route (the mcpService.* IPC channels stay denied to remote callers).
   128	        const removeResponse = isElectronDesktop()
   129	          ? await mcpService.removeMcpFromAgents.invoke({
   130	              mcpServerName: serverName,
   131	              agents: agentsResponse.data,
   132	            })
   133	          : await removeMcpFromAgentsHttp(serverName);
   134	        await handleMcpOperationResult(removeResponse, 'remove', successMessage, true); // Skip re-detection
   135	        const removalResults = removeResponse.data?.results ?? [];
   136	        const failedRemovals = removalResults.filter((result) => !result.success);
   137	        if (!removeResponse.success || removalResults.length === 0 || failedRemovals.length > 0) {
   138	          throw new Error(
   139	            removeResponse.msg ||
   140	              failedRemovals.map((result) => `${result.agent}: ${result.error || 'removal failed'}`).join(', ') ||
   141	              t('settings.mcpRemoveFailed')
   142	          );
   143	        }
   144	        return removeResponse.data?.results ?? [];
   145	      } else {
   120	    try {
   121	      const result = await mcpService.testMcpConnection(server);
   122	      return { success: true, data: result };
   123	    } catch (error) {
   124	      return {
   125	        success: false,
   126	        msg: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error testing MCP connection',
   127	      };
   128	    }
   129	  });
   130	
   131	  ipcBridge.mcpService.syncMcpToAgents.provider(async ({ mcpServers, agents }) => {
   132	    try {
   133	      const result = await mcpService.syncMcpToAgents(mcpServers, agents);
   134	      return { success: true, data: result };
   135	    } catch (error) {
   136	      return {

   139	      };
   140	    }
   141	  });
   142	
   143	  ipcBridge.mcpService.removeMcpFromAgents.provider(async ({ mcpServerName, agents }) => {
   144	    try {
   145	      const result = await mcpService.removeMcpFromAgents(mcpServerName, agents);
   146	      return { success: true, data: result };
   147	    } catch (error) {
   148	      return {
   149	        success: false,
   150	        msg: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error removing MCP from agents',
   151	      };
   152	    }
   153	  });

   251	            server.transport.type === 'sse' ||
   252	            server.transport.type === 'http' ||
   253	            server.transport.type === 'streamable_http'
   254	          ) {
   255	            // Handle SSE/HTTP/Streamable HTTP transport types
   256	            // Claude CLI uses --transport http for both HTTP and Streamable HTTP
   257	            // Format: claude mcp add -s user --transport <type> <name> <url> [--header ...]
   258	            const transportFlag = server.transport.type === 'streamable_http' ? 'http' : server.transport.type;
   259	            // Pass name/url/headers as separate argv elements (shell:false) so
   260	            // shell metacharacters in any value cannot inject commands (SEC-MCP-01).

   263	            // Add headers
   264	            if (server.transport.headers) {
   265	              for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(server.transport.headers)) {
   266	                args.push('--header', `${key}: ${value}`);
   267	              }
   268	            }
   269	
   270	            try {
   271	              await safeExecFile('claude', args, {
   272	                timeout: 5000,
   273	                ...getExecEnv(),
   274	              });
   275	              console.log(`[ClaudeMcpAgent] Added MCP server: ${server.name}`);
   276	            } catch (error) {
   277	              const detail = execErrorDetail(error);
   278	              console.warn(`Failed to add MCP ${server.name} to Claude Code: ${detail}`);
   279	              failures.push(`${server.name}: ${detail}`);
   280	            }
   281	          } else {
   282	            failures.push(
   283	              `${server.name}: Claude Code does not support ${(server.transport as { type: string }).type} transport type`
   284	            );
   285	          }
   286	        }
   287	        return failures.length === 0 ? { success: true } : { success: false, error: failures.join('; ') };
   288	      } catch (error) {
   289	        return { success: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };
   290	      }
   291	    };
   292	
   293	    Object.defineProperty(installOperation, 'name', { value: 'installMcpServers' });
   294	    return this.withLock(installOperation);
   295	  }
   296	
   297	  /**
   298	   * Remove an MCP server from the Claude Code agent
   299	   */
   300	  removeMcpServer(mcpServerName: string): Promise<McpOperationResult> {
   301	    const removeOperation = async () => {
   302	      try {
   303	        // Use Claude CLI command to remove MCP server (try different scopes)
   304	        // Order: user (Wayland default) -> local -> project
   305	        // user scope first, because Wayland installs use user scope
   306	        const scopes = ['user', 'local', 'project'] as const;
   307	        const candidateNames = Array.from(
   308	          new Set(
   309	            isBuiltinImageGenName(mcpServerName)
   310	              ? [mcpServerName, BUILTIN_IMAGE_GEN_NAME, ...BUILTIN_IMAGE_GEN_LEGACY_NAMES]
   311	              : [mcpServerName]
   312	          )
   313	        );
   314	        const failures: string[] = [];
   315	
   316	        for (const scope of scopes) {
   317	          for (const candidateName of candidateNames) {
   318	            try {
   319	              const result = await safeExecFile(
   320	                'claude',
   321	                ['mcp', 'remove', '-s', scope, cliSafeMcpServerName(candidateName)],
   322	                {
   323	                  timeout: 5000,
   324	                  ...getExecEnv(),
   325	                }
   326	              );
   327	
   328	              if (result.stdout && result.stdout.includes('removed')) {
   329	                console.log(`[ClaudeMcpAgent] Removed MCP server from ${scope} scope: ${candidateName}`);
   330	                return { success: true };
   331	              }
   332	            } catch (error) {
   333	              const detail = execErrorDetail(error);
   334	
   335	              if (isClaudeMcpAbsentDetail(detail)) {
   336	                continue;
   337	              }
   338	
   339	              console.warn(`[ClaudeMcpAgent] Failed to remove from ${scope} scope: ${detail}`);
   340	              failures.push(`${scope}/${candidateName}: ${detail}`);
   341	            }
   342	          }
   343	        }
   344	
   345	        if (failures.length > 0) return { success: false, error: failures.join('; ') };
   346	        // Every scope explicitly reported absence.
   347	        console.log(`[ClaudeMcpAgent] MCP server ${mcpServerName} not found in any scope (may already be removed)`);
   348	        return { success: true };
   349	      } catch (error) {
   350	        return { success: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };
   351	      }
   352	    };
   353	
   354	    Object.defineProperty(removeOperation, 'name', { value: 'removeMcpServer' });
   355	    return this.withLock(removeOperation);
   356	  }
   357	}

     6	
     7	import { resolveNpxPath, normalizeNpxArgsForBundledBun } from '@process/utils/shellEnv';
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Resolve a stored MCP stdio transport's runtime hint into an actually-spawnable
    11	 * command/args pair (#827).
    12	 *
    13	 * Catalog-installed MCP servers persist a bare runtime hint like `"npx"` as their
    14	 * transport command. The connection-TEST path (McpProtocol) already rewrites that
    15	 * to the bundled Bun runtime (`bun x --bun <pkg>`) before spawning — which is why
    16	 * the Library shows a green/connected badge. But the real SESSION-injection paths
    17	 * (ACP `session/new`, the wcore engine's config.toml, per-CLI configs) forwarded
    18	 * the raw `"npx"` verbatim. On Windows a bare `npx` is `npx.cmd` and does not
    19	 * resolve via `CreateProcess`/PATHEXT for a shell:false spawn. On macOS/Linux a
    20	 * bare command also reintroduces a dependency on the GUI process's PATH that
    21	 * the successful probe never exercised. Either split can yield "green, but no
    22	 * tools" in the live session.
    23	 *
    24	 * Resolve on every platform. The Library probe already executes `npx` servers
    25	 * through Wayland's bundled Bun; forwarding raw `npx` to the live session made
    26	 * the probe and the real launch depend on different runtimes. On macOS that is
    27	 * enough to produce a false green when Electron was opened from Finder and the
    28	 * user's nvm/fnm PATH is unavailable to the agent that owns the MCP child.
    29	 *
    30	 * `resolveNpx`/`platform` are injectable so the decision is unit-testable without
    31	 * a bundled Bun on disk or a real Windows host.
    32	 */
    33	export function resolveMcpStdioSpawn(
    34	  command: string,
    35	  args: readonly string[] = [],
    36	  resolveNpx: () => string = () => resolveNpxPath({}),
    37	  _platform: NodeJS.Platform = process.platform
    38	): { command: string; args: string[] } {
    39	  if (command === 'npx') {
    40	    return { command: resolveNpx(), args: ['x', '--bun', ...normalizeNpxArgsForBundledBun([...args])] };
    41	  }
    42	  return { command, args: [...args] };
    43	}
    44	
    45	/**
    46	 * Resolve a stdio command for a configuration that survives app restarts.
    47	 *
    48	 * Linux AppImage resource paths are mounted under a different temporary path
    49	 * on each launch, so an absolute bundled-Bun path must never be persisted into
    50	 * Core's config.toml. Core itself is launched with the current bundled Bun
    51	 * directory at the front of PATH, therefore the portable `bun x --bun` form is
    52	 * both spawnable and restart-safe. Windows install paths are stable and retain
    53	 * the absolute resolution used by the session path.
    54	 */
    55	export function resolvePersistedMcpStdioSpawn(
    56	  command: string,
    57	  args: readonly string[] = [],
    58	  resolveNpx: () => string = () => resolveNpxPath({}),
    59	  platform: NodeJS.Platform = process.platform
    60	): { command: string; args: string[] } {
    61	  const resolved = resolveMcpStdioSpawn(command, args, resolveNpx, platform);
    62	  if (command === 'npx' && platform !== 'win32') {
    63	    return { command: 'bun', args: resolved.args };
    64	  }
    65	  return resolved;
    66	}
